# ADCIRC 입력 파일 문서와 코드 대조

## 1 요약

대상 입력의 516개 비교 행을 판정했다.
불일치는 90개 비교 행이다.
수는 2절의 ID별 판정 행을 기준으로 집계했다.
같은 원인을 가진 여러 필드와 NWS 조합 값도 각각 셌다.
판정하지 못한 6개 항목은 U-01–U-06에 이유와 함께 남겼다.

| 판정 | 행 수 | 집계 근거 |
|---|---:|---|
| 일치 | 311 | 2절의 해당 판정 행 |
| 불일치 | 90 | 2절의 해당 판정 행 |
| 문서에만 | 7 | 2절의 해당 판정 행 |
| 코드에만 | 108 | 2절의 해당 판정 행 |
| 판정 보류 | 6 | U-01–U-06 |

NWS 표의 숫자 값 126개를 각각 코드 분기와 대조했다.
코드에만 있는 입력 목록은 85개 행이다.
노트 대조와 수정안은 37개 행이다.
각 수의 근거는 2.15절·3절·4절의 행이다.

| 대상 | 판정 행 수 | 불일치 행 수 | 불일치 ID |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| fort.14 | 32 | 5 | F14-12, F14-28, F14-29, F14-30, F14-32 |
| fort.13 | 52 | 11 | NA-01, NA-06, NA-09, NA-12, NA-13, NA-16, NA-17, NA-21, NA-28, NA-29, NA-36 |
| fort.10·11·11.nc | 18 | 1 | F11-08 |
| fort.18 | 30 | 0 | 없음 |
| fort.19 | 6 | 2 | F19-01, F19-05 |
| fort.20 | 7 | 2 | F20-01, F20-06 |
| fort.24·24.nc | 8 | 2 | F24-02, F24-04 |
| fort.35–39 | 18 | 4 | FORT36-01, FORT37-01, F39-01, F39-03 |
| fort.141 | 6 | 1 | BATH-01 |
| 시간가변 weir·schedule | 18 | 4 | WEIR-01, WEIR-06, WEIR-10, SCH-03 |
| fort.rotm | 4 | 1 | ROT-04 |
| *_stat.151 | 5 | 0 | 없음 |
| 시작 명령줄 옵션 | 27 | 4 | CLI-10, CLI-11, CLI-12, CLI-13 |
| NWS 값·조합 표 | 128 | 10 | NWS-12101, NWS-12102, NWS-12107, NWS-12301, NWS-12302, NWS-12307, NWS-12401, NWS-12402, NWS-12407, NWS-heading |
| fort.22의 기타 NWS 형식·단위 | 47 | 13 | MET-02, MET-05, MET-07, MET-09, MET-13, MET-16, MET-32, MET-34, MET-38, MET-42, MET-41, MET-45, MET-47 |
| fort.200 계열 | 10 | 5 | F200-05, F200-06, F200-07, F200-09, F200-10 |
| OWI ASCII NWS=±12 | 10 | 1 | OWI-06 |
| OWI netCDF NWS=±13 | 13 | 3 | NC13-02, NC13-08, NC13-12 |
| grb2·netCDF NWS=±14 | 14 | 5 | NC14-03, NC14-06, NC14-07, NC14-08, NC14-12 |
| fort.25·225·227·얼음 | 15 | 5 | ICE-01, ICE-02, ICE-06, ICE-10, ICE-11 |
| fort.23 | 9 | 1 | F23-01 |
| fort.67·68 핫스타트 | 33 | 10 | HS-04, HS-08, HS-09, HS-11, HS-12, HS-13, HS-16, HS-20, HS-21, HS-22 |
| subgrid lookup netCDF | 3 | 0 | 없음 |
| fort.14·13 XDMF 대체 형식 | 3 | 0 | 없음 |

불일치 ID의 원문과 이유는 2절의 같은 ID에 적었다.
불일치 ID의 현재 노트와 수정안은 4절에 연결했다.

이 문서는 AI가 작성한 원문 대조 기록이다.
코드 판 표기는 ADCIRC v56.2.1이다.
이 표기는 CMakeLists.txt의 버전 문자열을 따른다.
대조 대상은 이 저장소에 있는 원문이다.
판 근거는 `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/CMakeLists.txt:92`의 `set(ADCIRC_VERSION_STRING "v56.2.1")`이다.
문서의 우선순위는 코드 저장소의 rst, 위키, 웹 v53, PDF 순서다.
PDF 원문을 이 판정의 근거로 인용하지 않았다.
이 대조에는 PDF 식에 근거한 판정이 없다.
코드 판정은 `nl -ba`로 연 원문에 근거한다.
판독 기록은 위치를 찾는 색인으로만 사용했다.
문서가 같은 필드를 다르게 설명하면 해당 행에 두 문서를 인용했다.
웹 v53의 불일치는 v56.2.1과의 판 차이를 이유에 적었다.
‘일치’는 해당 행에 적은 형식·의미·단위·조건의 일치다.
‘코드에만’은 이 작업의 문서 범위에서 해당 입력 규약을 찾지 못했다는 뜻이다.
‘문서에만’은 문서의 규약을 대상 코드의 읽기 경로에서 찾지 못했다는 뜻이다.
읽기 규약이 같아도 코드가 문서의 모든 입력 제약을 검사한다는 뜻은 아니다.
한 레코드의 여러 필드는 같은 읽기문을 공유할 때 같은 행에 적었다.
노드 속성의 ‘초기값’은 코드의 초기화값이다.
노드 속성의 ‘파일 기본값’은 fort.13에서 사용자가 지정한 값이다.
두 값은 서로 다르다.
모델 실행과 컴파일은 수행하지 않았다.
git은 사용하지 않았다.
작성 범위는 이 파일 하나다.

| F14-18 | NBVV: 32,52,102,112,122 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:103` `= 32 a combined specified normal flux and outward radiating boundary. The GWCE is forced with the total normal flux computed by adding the specified normal flux and the flux associated with the outward radiating wave. The latter is determine from a Sommerfeld type condition, flux=celerity*wave elevation. The momentum equations are used to compute the velocity field the same as for a nonboundary node.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:109` `= 52 external boundary with periodic non-zero normal flow combined with wave radiation normal to the boundary as natural boundary conditions and no constraint on the tangential flow. This is applied by specifying the non-zero contribution to the normal boundary flux integral in the continuity equation. There is no constraint on velocity (normal or tangential) in the momentum equations. This boundary condition should correctly satisfy the flux balance in a global sense but will only satisfy the normal flow at each boundary node in the limit of infinite resolution. This type of boundary represents a periodic river inflow or open ocean boundary with a weak specified normal flow condition and free tangential slip where waves are allowed to propagate freely out of the domain. Discharges are specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File as harmonic discharge forcing. Additional parameters, including DRampExtFlux and FluxSettlingTime must also be set in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File in order to use this boundary type.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:113` `= 102, 112, or 122 flux specified baroclinic. In order to designate a river boundary as baroclinic, 100 should be added to the IBTYPE that would be appropriate in the barotropic case. For example, to convert a barotropic river boundary (IBTYPE of 22) to a baroclinic river boundary with freshwater inflow, change the IBTYPE to 122. If there is a 1 in the 100s place of the IBTYPE, ADCIRC will then try to read an input file (fort.39) for the salinity and/or temperature boundary condition. The format of the fort.39 file depends on the value of IDEN; see the documentation of the fort.39 file for more details.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:446` `case(0,1,2,10,11,12,20,21,22,30,32,52,102,112,122,152)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:449` `read(unit=iunit,fmt=*,err=10,end=20,iostat=ios) n1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1405` `case(102,112,122,152)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1422` `ibtype(k) = ibtype_orig(k) - 100` | 일치 | fort14.rst의 형식 목록은 이 값들을 빠뜨린다. parameter_definitions는 이 값들을 설명한다. 바록리닉 경계는 100을 빼서 유량 경계로 처리한다. |

The GWCE is forced with normal flux, the momentum eqs are sacrificed in favor of setting the velocity at a boundary node equal to the value at a fictitious point inside the domain. ### 2.5 fort.19/20: 비주기 경계

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| F19-01 | fort.19 읽기 조건 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:6` ``The fort.19 file contains non-periodic, time varying elevation boundary conditions for "elevation specified" boundary nodes. This file is only read when an "elevation specified" boundary condition has been specified in the :doc:`Grid and Boundary Information File <fort14>` (NOPE>0) and NBFR=0 in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`.`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3427` `IF (MNOPE.GT.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3429` `INQUIRE(FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.19',exist=NPEBC)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:198` `IF (NPEBC) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:207` `OPEN(19,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.19')` | 불일치 | 문서는 NOPE>0 및 NBFR=0을 모두 요구한다. 코드는 개방 경계가 있을 때 파일 존재 여부로 NPEBC를 설정한다. 이 검사에는 NBFR=0 조건이 없다. |
| F19-02 | ETIMINC | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:15` ``:ref:`ETIMINC <ETIMINC>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:1927` `Time increment (secs) between consecutive sets of elevation specified boundary condition values contained in this file.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:208` `READ(19,*) ETIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:216` `ETIME2 = ETIME1 + ETIMINC` | 일치 | 코드는 초 단위 시간 간격을 한 번 읽는다. |
| F19-03 | ESBIN 및 노드 순서 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:17` ``:ref:`ESBIN(k) <ESBIN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:27` `* The node order in this file must match the order specified in the elevation specified boundary condition part of the Grid and Boundary Information File.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:209` `DO J=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:210` `READ(19,*) ESBIN1(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:212` `DO J=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:213` `READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J)` | 일치 | 코드는 노드 번호 없이 경계 배열 순서로 수위를 읽는다. 코드에는 단위 변환이 없다. |
| F19-04 | 첫 시각·시간 범위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:25` `* The first set of elevation values are provided at TIME=STATIM (as specified in fort.15). Additional sets of elevation values are provided every ETIMINC.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:26` `* Enough sets of elevation values must be provided to extend for the entire model run, otherwise the run will crash!` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:215` `ETIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:216` `ETIME2 = ETIME1 + ETIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:873` `ETIME1=STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:881` `DO IT=1,ITHS-1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:883` `IF(TIMEIT.GT.ETIME2) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:888` `READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J)` | 일치 | 코드는 STATIM을 일에서 초로 바꾼다. 핫스타트는 필요한 시각까지 자료를 읽어 넘긴다. 읽기문에는 EOF 대체 자료가 없다. |
| F19-05 | ETIMINC 반복 여부 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort19.rst:20` `Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:208` `READ(19,*) ETIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:210` `READ(19,*) ESBIN1(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:213` `READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:884` `ETIME1=ETIME2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:885` `ETIME2=ETIME1+ETIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:888` `READ(19,*) ESBIN2(J)` | 불일치 | 문서의 ‘Repeat the block above’는 ETIMINC까지 포함한다. 코드는 파일 처음에서 ETIMINC를 한 번만 읽는다. 문서의 예제 36–45줄은 코드의 한 번 읽기와 맞는다. |
| F20-01 | 읽기 조건 NFFR | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:6` ``The fort.20 file contains non-periodic, normal flow boundary conditions for "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary nodes. This file is only read when a "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary condition has been specified in the :doc:`Grid and Boundary Information File <fort14>` (IBTYPE=2, 12, or 22) and NFFR=0 in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:811` `Number of frequencies in the specified normal flow external boundary condition. If NFFR=0 or NFFR=-1, the normal flow boundary condition is assumed to be non-periodic and will be read in from the Non-periodic, Normal Flux Boundary Condition File. If NFFR=0, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the cold start time; but if NFFR=-1, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the hot start time. On the other hand, positive integer values of NFFR indicate the number of frequency components that make up the periodic flux boundaries.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:226` `IF(NFLUXF.EQ.1.AND.NFFR.LE.0)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:981` `IF((NFLUXF.EQ.1).AND.(NFFR.EQ.-1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:987` `OPEN(20,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.20')` | 불일치 | fort20.rst는 NFFR=0만 적는다. 코드는 NFFR=-1의 핫스타트 기준 자료도 읽는다. parameter_definitions는 이 확장 조건을 설명한다. |
| F20-02 | FTIMINC | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:15` ``:ref:`FTIMINC <FTIMINC>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:1935` `Time increment (secs) between consecutive sets of normal flow boundary condition values contained in this file.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:233` `READ(20,*) FTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:256` `QTIME2 = QTIME1 + FTIMINC` | 일치 | 코드는 초 단위 시간 간격을 처음에 한 번 읽는다. |
| F20-03 | QNIN: 2,12,22; 순서·부호·단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:17` ``:ref:`QNIN(k) <QNIN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:27` `* The node order in this file must match the order specified in the normal flow boundary condition part of the Grid and Boundary Information File.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:28` `* A positive flow/unit width is into the domain and a negative flow/unit width is out of the domain.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:1945` `Normal flow/unit width (e.g., m2/s) at specified normal flow node k. A positive flow/unit width is into the domain and a negative flow/unit width is out of the domain. The sequencing is assumed to match what is defined in the part of the Grid and Boundary Information File specifying non zero normal flow boundaries.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:238` `IF ((LBCODEI(J).EQ.2).OR.(LBCODEI(J).EQ.12).OR.(LBCODEI(J).EQ.22)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:239` `READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:240` `QNIN1(J) = rotate_normal_flux(ICS, J, QNIN_TMP)` | 일치 | 코드는 선택한 경계 노드 순서로 단위 폭 유량을 읽는다. 코드는 좌표계에 따른 법선 유량 보정을 적용한다. |
| F20-04 | IBTYPE=32의 ENIN | fort20.rst의 필드 목록에는 ENIN이 없다. `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:17` ``:ref:`QNIN(k) <QNIN>` `` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:241` `ELSEIF (LBCODEI(J).EQ.32) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:242` `READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP,ENIN1(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:243` `QNIN1(J) = rotate_normal_flux(ICS, J, QNIN_TMP)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 32 경계에서 유량과 수위 두 값을 읽는다. |
| F20-05 | STATIM 및 핫스타트 시각 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:25` `* The first set of normal flow values is provided at TIME=STATIM (as specified in fort.15). Additional sets of normal flow values are provided every FTIMINC.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:811` `Number of frequencies in the specified normal flow external boundary condition. If NFFR=0 or NFFR=-1, the normal flow boundary condition is assumed to be non-periodic and will be read in from the Non-periodic, Normal Flux Boundary Condition File. If NFFR=0, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the cold start time; but if NFFR=-1, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the hot start time. On the other hand, positive integer values of NFFR indicate the number of frequency components that make up the periodic flux boundaries.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:255` `QTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:256` `QTIME2 = QTIME1 + FTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:989` `QTIME1=TIMELOC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/hstart.F:990` `QTIME2=QTIME1+FTIMINC` | 일치 | NFFR=0의 시간 기준은 STATIM이다. NFFR=-1의 시간 기준은 TIMELOC이다. |
| F20-06 | FTIMINC 반복 여부 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst:20` `Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:233` `READ(20,*) FTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:239` `READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:248` `READ(20,*) QNIN_TMP` | 불일치 | 코드는 FTIMINC를 파일 처음에서 한 번만 읽는다. 반복 자료는 유량 레코드다. |

### 2.6 fort.24: 자가인력·지구하중 조석

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| F24-01 | NTIP=2 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:6` ``Self attraction/earth load tides are used to force ADCIRC when NTIP=2 in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`.`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6367` `IF(NTIP.EQ.2) THEN` | 일치 | 코드는 NTIP.EQ.2 조건에서 fort.24 입력 처리를 시작한다. |
| F24-02 | Alpha line | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:18` `Alpha line`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:30` `1. The first four lines (Alpha line, Constituent frequency, 1, Constituent name) for each constituent must be present in the file but they are skipped over during the ADCIRC read.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6468` `READ(24, *) dmy_char, dmy_char` | 불일치 | 문서는 알파 한 레코드를 건너뛴다고 적는다. 코드는 같은 읽기문에서 문자값 두 개를 요구한다. 공백 없는 단일 값 레코드는 다음 레코드까지 소비할 수 있다. |
| F24-03 | Constituent frequency, 1 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:19` `Constituent frequency`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:20` `1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:30` `1. The first four lines (Alpha line, Constituent frequency, 1, Constituent name) for each constituent must be present in the file but they are skipped over during the ADCIRC read.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6469` `READ(24, *) dmy_real`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6470` `READ(24, *) dmy_int` | 일치 | 코드는 주파수 실수와 정수 레코드를 읽는다. 코드는 이 값을 조석 주파수로 사용하지 않는다. |
| F24-04 | Constituent name·순서 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:21` `Constituent name (e.g., M2)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:30` `1. The first four lines (Alpha line, Constituent frequency, 1, Constituent name) for each constituent must be present in the file but they are skipped over during the ADCIRC read.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:32` ``2. Entries must be grouped by constituents and must be in the same order as the tidal potential terms listed in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`.`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6471` `READ(24, '(A)') const_name`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6477` `IF(TRIM(TIPOTAG(J)).EQ.TRIM(const_name))THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6478` `IDX = J`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6482` `IF(IDX.EQ.-1)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6486` `call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage)` | 불일치 | 문서는 성분 이름을 건너뛰고 fort.15와 같은 순서를 요구한다. 모델 ASCII reader는 이름으로 TIPOTAG를 찾는다. 모델 reader는 알 수 없는 이름에서 종료한다. 해석: 모델 reader에는 문서의 동일 순서 조건이 필요하지 않다. prep의 순서 검사는 F24-08에 적었다. |
| F24-05 | JN,SALTAMP,SALTPHA | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort24.rst:23` ``:ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`SALTAMP(k,JN) <SALTAMP>`, :ref:`SALTPHA(k,JN) <SALTPHA>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:2120` `Amplitude of the self attraction/earth tide loading forcing for constituent k and node number JN. If ICS=1, units are determined by the units of gravity (G) specified in the fort.15 file (eg. if G is in m/s2, amplitude is in m, if gravity is in ft/s2, amplitude is in ft.). If ICS=2, gravity must be specified in m/s2 and the amplitude is in m.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:2125` `Phase (degrees) of the self attraction/earth tide loading forcing for constituent k and node number JN.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6491` `READ(24,*) JJ,SALTAMP(IDX,find(node_dict,JJ)),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6492` `&                        SALTPHA(IDX,find(node_dict,JJ))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6496` `SALTPHA = SALTPHA*DEG2RAD` | 일치 | 코드는 노드 라벨을 매핑한다. 코드는 위상을 도에서 라디안으로 바꾼다. 코드는 진폭을 그대로 저장한다. |

### 2.7 fort.13: 속성별 의미·단위·기본값

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

각 속성의 파일 기본값은 fort.13 헤더의 DefaultAttrValues다. 문서는 속성별 고정 파일 기본값을 정하지 않는다. 아래 이유의 초기값은 코드 초기화값이다. Units 문자열은 수치 변환을 실행하지 않는다.

| NA-01 | primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation: 단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:64` `**Description**: Same as existing TAU0.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:68` `**Units**: Unitless. (Units for nodal attributes are specified by the user).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:70` `**Number of values per node**: 1.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md:180` `Units: Specified by the user, 1/time.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1214` `READ(13,'(A80)') Tau0Units`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1215` `READ(13,*) Tau0NoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1216` `READ(13,*) Tau0DefVal`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:497` `Tau0DefVal             = 0.0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1919` `&           /,5X,'Tau0 = ',E15.8,2X,'1/sec',/)` | 불일치 | [10-10 검증 정정] rst는 'Unitless'라고 적고 같은 줄에 단위는 사용자가 지정한다고 적는다. 위키는 'Specified by the user, 1/time.'이라고 적는다. 불일치는 rst와 위키 사이의 차이이다. 코드는 Tau0Units를 A80 문자열로 읽기만 한다(nodalattr.F:1214). nodalattr.F:1919의 '1/sec' 로그는 노드 속성을 읽지 않을 때(.not.LoadTau0) 쓰는 fort.15 상수 Tau0의 로그이므로 노드 속성 단위의 근거가 아니다. 초기값은 0이다. |
| NA-02 | primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation: 우선순위·음수 기본값 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:66` `**Requirement**: Required, but not necessarily in the fort.13 file as there is a choice of specification methods. Can be specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File as a positive constant, in which case it is spatially uniform; or a negative constant, in which case it is spatially varying according to a hardcoded scheme based on depth. Can also be specified as a nodal attribute in the Nodal Attributes File (fort.13), in which case any value specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File is ignored (nodal attributes take precedence).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1373` `CALL LoadAttrVec(TAU0VAR, Tau0DefVal,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1952` `IF (LoadTau0.and.Tau0DefVal.lt.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1954` `IF (Tau0Var(I).lt.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1955` `Tau0Var(I)=Tau0NodalValue(Tau0DefVal,DP(I))` | 일치 | 선택한 속성이 Tau0Var를 채운다. 음수 파일 기본값을 지정하면 음수 노드값을 수심 기반 함수로 바꾼다. 이 조건은 문서의 우선순위 설명에 추가되는 코드 규약이다. |
| NA-03 | min_and_max_primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:80` `**Description**: Controls upper and lower bounds on TAU0, which affects numerical damping and stability.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:82` `**Requirement**: Optional.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:84` `**Units**: Specified by the user, 1/time.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:88` `**ADCIRC Variable**: Tau0MinMax` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:489` `Tau0MinMaxNoOfVals    = 2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1284` `READ(13,'(A80)') Tau0MinMaxUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1285` `READ(13,*) Tau0MinMaxNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1286` `READ(13,*) (Tau0MinMaxDefVal(j),j=1,Tau0MinMaxNoOfVals)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1472` `CALL LoadAttrMat(Tau0MinMax, Tau0MinMaxNoOfVals,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1981` `Tau0MinMax(I,1) = Tau0FullDomainMin`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1982` `Tau0MinMax(I,2) = Tau0FullDomainMax` | 일치 | 코드는 하한과 상한 두 값을 읽는다. 속성을 선택하지 않은 Tau0=-5 경로는 설정된 전역 하한·상한을 복사한다. |

| NA-07 | surface_directional_effective_roughness_length | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:126` `**Description**: A measure of the "roughness" of the land that can impede wind flow and reduce the surface stress that the wind applies. The ocean would be considered very smooth, and skyscrapers would be considered very rough. This attribute is directional, and the twelve values represent the roughness lengths "seen" by winds blowing from twelve different compass directions at each node. The orientation of the twelve values follows the trigonometric convention, that is, zero degrees represents due east, and the values proceed counter clockwise. | NA-11 | mannings_n_at_sea_floor: 입력·변환 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:174` `**Description**: Manning's n. If the user elects to use this nodal attribute, NOLIBF must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. During execution, the Manning's n value specified here is converted to an equivalent quadratic friction coefficient before the bottom stress is calculated. The equivalent quadratic friction coefficient is calculated according to the following formula at each node at each time step: Cd(t)=(g*n^2)/cuberoot(depth[+eta(t)]) where depth is the bathymetric depth and [eta(t)] is the water surface elevation. The addition of the water surface elevation is conditional upon the setting of NOLIFA: eta(t) is treated as zero if NOLIFA is set to zero in the fort.15 file. Finally, the value of CF in the fort.15 is used to set a lower limit on the resulting equivalent quadratic friction coefficient, since the Cd calculated from this formula tends to become small in deep water.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:178` `**Units**: Specified by user.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:180` `**Number of values per node**: 1.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:182` `**Values**: Greater than zero.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1252` `READ(13,'(A80)') ManningsNUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1253` `READ(13,*) ManningsNNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1254` `READ(13,*) ManningsNDefVal`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:511` `ManningsNDefVal        = 0.0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2387` `FRIC(I) = g*ManningsN(I)**2.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2388` `&               /((DP(I)+IFNLFA*ETA2(I))**(1.d0/3.d0))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2424` `FRIC(I)=g*(WPFac*ManningsN(I))**2.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2425` `&                    /( ( HT )**(1.d0/3.d0) ) ! sb46.28sb02`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2427` `IF(FRIC(I).LT.BFCdLLimit) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2428` `FRIC(I) = BFCdLLimit` | 일치 | 코드는 값 하나를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 일반 경로는 전체 수심의 세제곱근으로 나눈 이차 마찰계수를 만든다. CF 기반 하한을 적용한다. 젖은 둘레 보정은 코드에 추가로 있다. |
| NA-12 | mannings_n_at_sea_floor: NOLIBF·0 이하 값 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:174` `**Description**: Manning's n. If the user elects to use this nodal attribute, NOLIBF must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. During execution, the Manning's n value specified here is converted to an equivalent quadratic friction coefficient before the bottom stress is calculated. The equivalent quadratic friction coefficient is calculated according to the following formula at each node at each time step: Cd(t)=(g*n^2)/cuberoot(depth[+eta(t)]) where depth is the bathymetric depth and [eta(t)] is the water surface elevation. The addition of the water surface elevation is conditional upon the setting of NOLIFA: eta(t) is treated as zero if NOLIFA is set to zero in the fort.15 file. Finally, the value of CF in the fort.15 is used to set a lower limit on the resulting equivalent quadratic friction coefficient, since the Cd calculated from this formula tends to become small in deep water.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:182` `**Values**: Greater than zero.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1850` `IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1851` `&     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2423` `IF (ManningsN(I).le.0d0) cycle ! | NA-13 | chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:189` `**Description**: Chezy friction coefficient. | NA-19 | elemental_slope_limiter | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:249` `**Description**: This nodal attribute is used to selectively limit the maximum elevation gradient that can occur across an element, thus improving numerical stability. Alternatively, it is also capable of merely logging individual elements where a specified elemental slope is exceeded at some point during the simulation. When this nodal attribute is loaded, warning messages will be written to the screen and to the fort.16 log file whenever the elevation gradient meets or exceeds the threshold value for the first time at a particular node. Once the elevation gradient is met or exceeded at a node, the elemental slope limiter remains active at that node for the remainder of the ADCIRC run. When the elemental slope limiter is active at a node, the water surface elevation at that node is reset to the average of the water surface elevations of the surrounding nodes. At the end of the run, a file called ESLNodes.63 will be written to indicate the nodes where the threshold elevation gradient was met or exceeded. Furthermore, If there is an ESLNodes.63 file in the input directory when ADCIRC starts, ADCIRC will load the existing ESLNodes.63 file along with the hotstart file so that the simulation can pick up where it left off, in terms of the elements where the slope is actively limited. When such a simulation finishes, it will overwrite the existing ESLNodes.63 file with a new one that reflects the updated state of limited elemental slopes.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:253` `**Units**: length/length or unitless.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:255` `**Number of values per node**: 1.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:257` `**Values**: Zero indicates that slope limiting is always active at that node, because a zero elevation gradient will always be met or exceeded; a positive value indicates the maximum gradient to be allowed at that node, at or beyond which the slope limiter is activated; a negative number indicates that the elevation gradients should be compared to the absolute value of the nodal attribute at this node. In the case of a negative number, ADCIRC will log a warning to the screen and to the fort.16 file the first time the elemental slope is exceeded, but ESLNodes.63 file will not be affected, and the elemental slope limiting will not actually occur at the node. A suggested value for this nodal attribute is 0.001.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1309` `READ(13,'(A80)') EleSlopeLimUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1310` `READ(13,*) EleSlopeLimNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1311` `READ(13,*) EleSlopeLimDefVal`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:519` `EleSlopeLimDefVal      = 0D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1505` `CALL LoadAttrVec(elemental_slope_limiter_grad_max,` | 일치 | 무차원 기울기 기준값 하나를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 값의 부호에 따른 소비 조건과 ESLNodes.63 읽기는 NA-29에 적었다. |
| NA-20 | advection_state | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:266` `**Description**: The advection_state nodal attribute is used to provide fine grained control over the NOLICA and NOLICAT parameters, so that they can be set on an element-by-element basis. The bathymetric depth at the three nodes of an element are compared to the corresponding nodal attribute values, and if the bathymetric depth at all three nodes is greater than or equal to the corresponding nodal attribute value, the values of NOLICA and NOLICAT will be set to the value indicated in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition (fort.15) file. If the bathymetric depth at any of the three nodes of a particular element is less than the corresponding nodal attribute value, the values of NOLICA and NOLICAT will be set to zero on that element.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:270` `**Units**: Length.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:272` `**Number of values per node**: 1.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:274` `**Values**: Any.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1296` `READ(13,'(A80)') AdvectionStateUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1297` `READ(13,*) AdvectionStateNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1298` `READ(13,*) AdvectionStateDefVal`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:427` `AdvectionStateDefVal   = 0.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3024` `IF ((DP(NM1).GE.AdvectionState(NM1)).AND.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3025` `&    (DP(NM2).GE.AdvectionState(NM2)).AND.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3026` `&    (DP(NM3).GE.AdvectionState(NM3))) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3027` `IFNLCT = IFNLCTE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3028` `IFNLCAT = IFNLCATE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3030` `IFNLCT = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3031` `IFNLCAT = 0` | 일치 | 길이 기준값 하나를 읽는다. 초기값은 0이다. 세 노드가 모두 기준값 이상이면 전역 이류 설정을 사용한다. 나머지 경우에는 두 설정을 0으로 바꾼다. |
| NA-21 | initial_river_elevation | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:283` ``**Description**: The initial_river_elevation nodal attribute is used to set the initial water surface elevation in inland rivers that have some portion of the river bed above mean sea level as well as an upstream flux boundary condition. ADCIRC assumes by default that vertices that are above mean sea level (i.e., with negative depths) will be dry when the simulation starts. This assumption is violated when the elevation of a river bed is above mean sea level, and there is an upstream flux boundary. | NA-24 | condensed_nodes | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:347` `**Description**: The condensed_nodes nodal attribute is used to specify a group of nodes, the nodal equations of which are to be condensed, and thus the solutions of which become identical. This condensation technique is useful when two or more nodes are within a close proximity and thus the model violates the CFL condition unless a smaller time step is used. The condensation is conducted at the level of nodal equations, which effectively extends the stencil of the nodes to relax the CFL condition. One typical use of the condensation is to relax the CFL condition along very narrow channels. The node pairs on the sides of a channel are specified as the condensed_nodes nodal attributes so that the model becomes insensitive to the width of the channel.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:351` `**Units**: Unitless.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:353` ``**Number of values per node**: Maximum number of nodes in a condensed node group. See :ref:`Example Fort.13 with Condensed Nodes <example_fort13_condensed_nodes>` for more information.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:355` `**Values**: Node numbers.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:357` `**ADCIRC Variable**: CondensedNodes.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1301` `READ(13,'(A80)') CondensedNodesUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1302` `READ(13,*) CondensedNodesNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1304` `ALLOCATE(CondensedNodesDefVal(CondensedNodesNoOfVals))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1305` `READ(13,*) (CondensedNodesDefVal(j),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1306` `&                  j=1,CondensedNodesNoOfVals)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1496` `CALL LoadAttrMatNodeNo(CondensedNodes,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1777` `READ(13,*) NodeNum, (TempAttData(j),j=1,NumCol)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1778` `DictNode = find(node_dict,NodeNum)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1779` `DO j=1,NumCol`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1780` `TempAttData(j) = find(node_dict,TempAttData(j))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1785` `AttributeData(DictNode,:) = TempAttData(:)` | 일치 | 노드 번호 벡터를 읽는다. 기본 벡터와 비기본 노드별 벡터를 파일에서 지정한다. 코드는 노드 라벨을 내부 번호로 매핑한다. |

| NA-31 | swan_local_control | 대상 문서에서 속성별 설명을 찾지 못했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1235` `READ(13,'(A80)') SwanLocalControlUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1236` `READ(13,*) SwanLocalControlNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1238` `&      (SwanLocalControlDefVal(j),j=1,SwanLocalControlNoOfVals)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:480` `SwanLocalControlNoOfVals = 2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:503` `SwanLocalControlDefVal(1) = 1.0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:504` `SwanLocalControlDefVal(2) = 0.0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1403` `CALL LoadAttrMat(SwanLocalControl,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2040` `IF(.not.LoadSwanLocalControl) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2046` `SwanLocalControl(I,J)=SwanLocalControlDefVal(J)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 두 값을 읽는다. 코드 주석은 SwanOn과 IntSource를 적는다. 초기값은 각각 1과 0이다. 선택하지 않은 경우에도 이 초기 벡터를 채운다. SWAN 내부 소비는 범위 밖이다. |
| NA-32 | overland_reduction_factor | 대상 문서에서 속성별 설명을 찾지 못했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1325` `READ(13,'(A80)') OverlandReductionFactorUnits`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1326` `READ(13,*) OverlandReductionFactorNoOfVals`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1327` `READ(13,*) OverlandReductionFactorDefaultValue`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1003` `OverlandReductionFactor(:) = 1D0/30D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1530` `CALL LoadAttrVec(OverlandReductionFactor,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2962` `TotalDepth = BathymetricDepth + Elevation`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2963` `if( (TotalDepth.gt.2D0*h0).and.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2964` `&      (BathymetricDepth.lt.CutOffDepth)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2965` `z0l=z0l-TotalDepth*OverlandReductionFactor(NodeNumber)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 값 하나를 읽는다. 배열 초기값은 1D0/30D0이다. 조건을 만족하면 전체 수심에 이 계수를 곱한 값을 육상 거칠기 길이에서 뺀다. 해석: 길이/길이 비율이다. |

| NA-35 | internal_tide_friction: 외부 3D 결합 확장 | 대상 문서는 노드당 1 또는 3 값만 설명한다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2656` `Baroc: IF (CBaroclinic.and.abs(IDEN).ge.5) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2661` `IF (IT_FricNoOfVals == 5) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2663` `C           1st column is coefficient, 2nd and 3rd columns are the Hx and Hy slopes,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2664` `C           4th and 5th columns are the Jx and Jy slopes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2673` `Fit = IT_Fric(I,1)*NB(I)/DP(I)*`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2674` `&                     sqrt(1d0 - CORIF(I)**2/omega**2)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2681` `TKM(1,I) = TK(I) + 2*Fit*IT_Fric(I,2)*IT_Fric(I,4)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2682` `TKM(2,I) = TK(I) + 2*Fit*IT_Fric(I,3)*IT_Fric(I,5)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2683` `TKM(3,I) = Fit*(IT_Fric(I,2)*IT_Fric(I,5) +`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2684` `&                               IT_Fric(I,3)*IT_Fric(I,4))` | 코드에만 | 코드는 외부 3D 결합에서 5개 성분의 소비 분기를 갖는다. 헤더 기본값 배열 선언은 길이 3이다. 해석: 5개 성분의 ASCII 입력 안전성은 이 소비 분기만으로 보장할 수 없다. 검증하지 않은 부분을 4절에 남겼다. |

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |

| F35-01 | fort.35 사용 조건 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort35.rst:6` ```The Level of No Motion Boundary Condition Input File (``fort.35``) is used in 3D baroclinic simulations when the :ref:`BCFLAG_LNM <BCFLAG_LNM>` parameter is set to 1 in the ``fort.15`` file. This file specifies elevation changes for ocean boundary nodes.``` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:835` `IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.GE.1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:836` `IF ((ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).GE.1).AND.(NOPE.GT.0)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:837` `IF (BCFLAG_LNM.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:838` `OPEN(35,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.35')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:940` `ELSE IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.LE.-1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:941` `IF ((RES_BC_FLAG.LT.0).AND.(NOPE.GT.0)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:942` `IF (BCFLAG_LNM.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:943` `OPEN(35,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.35')` | 일치 | 3D baroclinic·BCFLAG_LNM=1 경로에서 읽는다. 양수 IDEN은 시간 보간용 두 데이터셋을 읽는다. 음수 IDEN 경로는 한 데이터셋을 읽는다. |
| F35-02 | 날짜 주석·k·elevation_change | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort35.rst:16` `comment line (date)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort35.rst:18` `k, elevation_change`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort35.rst:25` `- The elevation changes should be specified for all ocean boundary nodes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort35.rst:26` `- Each dataset should include a comment line with the date` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:841` `READ(35,'(A)') CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:842` `DO NumofBCNodes=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:843` `READ(35,*) NOD,LNM_BC1(NumofBCNodes)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:845` `READ(35,'(A)') CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:846` `DO NumofBCNodes=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:847` `READ(35,*) NOD,LNM_BC2(NumofBCNodes)` | 일치 | 각 데이터셋에서 주석 줄 다음 NETA개 값을 읽는다. NOD를 배열 인덱스로 사용하지 않는다. 레코드 순서로 경계값을 채운다. 날짜 주석을 시간값으로 해석하지 않는다. |

This file specifies salinity values for ocean boundary nodes at different vertical levels.```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort36.rst:25` ``- This file is only required when running simulations with :ref:`RES_BC_FLAG <RES_BC_FLAG>` = -2, 2, -4, or 4`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:835` `IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.GE.1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:860` `IF ((ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.2).OR.(ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.4)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:862` `OPEN(36,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.36')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:940` `ELSE IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.LE.-1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:941` `IF ((RES_BC_FLAG.LT.0).AND.(NOPE.GT.0)) THEN` | 불일치 | 문서는 RES_BC_FLAG의 부호만으로 사용 조건을 설명한다. 코드는 양수 IDEN·3D baroclinic·해양 경계 존재 조건도 요구한다. 음수 IDEN 경로는 이 파일을 읽지 않는다. |

| FORT37-01 | fort37: 사용 조건 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:6` ```The Temperature Boundary Condition Input File (``fort.37``) is used when the :ref:`RES_BC_FLAG <RES_BC_FLAG>` parameter is set to -3, 3, -4, or 4 in the ``fort.15`` file. This file specifies temperature values for ocean boundary nodes at different vertical levels.```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:25` ``- This file is only required when running simulations with :ref:`RES_BC_FLAG <RES_BC_FLAG>` = -3, 3, -4, or 4`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:835` `IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.GE.1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:875` `IF ((ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.3).OR.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:878` `OPEN(37,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.37')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:940` `ELSE IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.LE.-1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:941` `IF ((RES_BC_FLAG.LT.0).AND.(NOPE.GT.0)) THEN` | 불일치 | 문서는 RES_BC_FLAG의 부호만으로 사용 조건을 설명한다. 코드는 양수 IDEN·3D baroclinic·해양 경계 존재 조건도 요구한다. 음수 IDEN 경로는 이 파일을 읽지 않는다. |
| FORT37-02 | 날짜 주석·k·TEMPBC(1:NFEN) | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:16` `comment line (date)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:18` ``k, (TEMPBC(k,m), m=1, :ref:`NFEN <NFEN>`)``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:26` `- The temperature values should be specified for all ocean boundary nodes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:27` `- Each dataset should include a comment line with the date`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:28` `- TEMPBC(k,m) represents the temperature value at node k and vertical level m`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort37.rst:29` `- NFEN is the number of vertical levels in the model` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:881` `READ(37,'(A)') CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:882` `DO NumofBCNodes=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:883` `READ(37,*) NOD,(RESTEMP1(NumofBCNodes,J),J=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:885` `READ(37,'(A)') CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:886` `DO NumofBCNodes=1,NETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:887` `READ(37,*) NOD,(RESTEMP2(NumofBCNodes,J),J=1,NFEN)` | 일치 | 코드는 주석 줄과 각 경계 노드의 NFEN개 수직 값을 읽는다. 노드 식별 필드는 순서 확인용으로 소비하지 않는다. 값을 별도 단위 변환 없이 저장한다. |
| FORT37-03 | 데이터 시간 | 전용 문서는 시간 기준 필드와 수치 단위를 따로 적지 않는다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:879` `TBCTIME1=TBCSTATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:880` `TBCTIME2=TBCTIME1+TBCTIMEINC` | 코드에만 | 시작 시간에 86400.D0을 곱한다. 증분은 해당 경계 시간 증분을 사용한다. |
| F38-01 | fort.38 사용 조건 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:6` ```The Surface Temperature Boundary Values Input File (``fort.38``) is used when the :ref:`RES_BC_FLAG <RES_BC_FLAG>` parameter is set to -3, 3, -4, or 4 in the ``fort.15`` file (i.e., when a lateral temperature boundary condition is being used). The format of this file depends on the :ref:`BCFLAG_TEMP <BCFLAG_TEMP>` parameter in the ``fort.15``, which controls the surface heat flux parameterization in ADCIRC.```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:42` ``- This file is only required when running simulations with :ref:`RES_BC_FLAG <RES_BC_FLAG>` = -3, 3, -4, or 4`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:835` `IF ((CBAROCLINIC).AND.(IDEN.GE.1)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:875` `IF ((ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.3).OR.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:876` `&            (ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.4)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:890` `IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.EQ.1) THEN ! read in file with multiple values but no calcs`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:891` `OPEN(38,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.38')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:900` `ELSE IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.EQ.2) THEN ! read in from file with 6 components`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:905` `READ(38,*) (NOD,(TMP(K,J),J=1,6),K=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:920` `ELSE IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.EQ.3) THEN ! read in from file with 4 components`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:925` `READ(38,*) (NOD,(TMP(K,J),J=1,4),K=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5621` `IF ((ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.3).OR.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5622` `&       (ABS(RES_BC_FLAG).EQ.4)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5623` `IF (BCFLAG_TEMP.NE.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5624` `READ(15,*) TTBCTIMEINC, TTBCSTATIM` | 일치 | 코드는 ABS(RES_BC_FLAG)=3 또는 4 안에서 BCFLAG_TEMP=1·2·3의 형식을 선택한다. 이 읽기 경로는 양수 IDEN·baroclinic 조건도 요구한다. NOPE>0 검사는 fort.37에 적용된다. fort.38 읽기는 그 검사 밖에 있다. |
| F38-02 | BCFLAG_TEMP=1: k·q_heat | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:13` `1. NOD를 인덱스로 쓰지 않는다. q_heat를 직접 저장한다. |
| F38-03 | BCFLAG_TEMP=2: k·TMP(1:6) | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:23` `2. If BCFLAG_TEMP=2:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:28` ``(K, (TMP(K,J), J=1,6),K=1, :ref:`NP <NP>`)``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:45` `- TMP(K,J) represents the Jth heat flux component for the Kth horizontal mesh node`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:46` `- The data are read using an implicit Fortran i/o loop, hence the parentheses`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort38.rst:47` `- BCFLAG_TEMP=2 requires 6 heat flux components` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:905` `READ(38,*) (NOD,(TMP(K,J),J=1,6),K=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:907` `q_heat1(NumofNodes)=-TMP(NumofNodes,1)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:908` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,2)+TMP(NumofNodes,3)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:909` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,5)+TMP(NumofNodes,4)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:910` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,6)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:912` `READ(38,*) (NOD,(TMP(K,J),J=1,6),K=1,NP)` | 일치 | 코드는 NP개 식별 필드와 여섯 성분을 implicit loop로 읽는다. |

| F38-05 | TMP 성분의 부호 조합 | 전용 문서는 성분별 부호 조합을 적지 않는다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:907` `q_heat1(NumofNodes)=-TMP(NumofNodes,1)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:908` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,2)+TMP(NumofNodes,3)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:909` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,5)+TMP(NumofNodes,4)-`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:910` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,6)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:927` `q_heat1(NumofNodes)=TMP(NumofNodes,4)+`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:928` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,3)-TMP(NumofNodes,1)+`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:929` `&              TMP(NumofNodes,2)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 인용한 식으로 q_heat를 계산한다. 성분별 물리량과 단위는 읽기문만으로 확정하지 않았다. 해당 항목을 4절의 미판정 목록에 적었다. |
| F39-01 | fort.39 사용 경계·IDEN | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:6` ```The Salinity and Temperature River Boundary Values Input File (``fort.39``) is used when the mesh file (``fort.14``) contains a baroclinic river boundary (IBTYPE=122) and :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` is positive.```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:30` ``- This file is only required when using baroclinic river boundaries (IBTYPE=122) with positive :ref:`IDEN <IDEN>` values`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1405` `case(102,112,122,152)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1406` `bndBCRiver=.true.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1422` `ibtype(k) = ibtype_orig(k) - 100`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:961` `IF (BndBCRiver .AND. (IDEN.EQ.2 .OR. IDEN.EQ.3 .OR.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:962` `&    IDEN.EQ.4)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:963` `OPEN(39,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.39',` | 불일치 | 코드는 IBTYPE 102·112·122·152를 river 경계로 분류한다. 읽기 조건은 IDEN=2·3·4다. 문서는 122와 모든 양수 IDEN으로 설명한다. 경계 배열 할당의 추가 제한은 F14-30에 적었다. |
| F39-02 | RIVBCTIMINC | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:15` `RIVBCTIMINC, RIVBCSTATIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:24` `- RIVBCTIMINC is the time increment (in seconds) between the boundary condition datasets` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:965` `READ(39,*) RIVBCTIMINC, RIVBCSTATIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:967` `RIVBCTIME2=RIVBCTIME1+RIVBCTIMINC` | 일치 | 데이터 시간 증분을 초로 더한다. |
| F39-03 | RIVBCSTATIM | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:25` `- RIVBCSTATIM is the time (in seconds) when the boundary condition data start, relative to the cold start time` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:965` `READ(39,*) RIVBCTIMINC, RIVBCSTATIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:966` `RIVBCTIME1=RIVBCSTATIM*86400.d0` | 불일치 | 문서는 초를 적는다. 코드는 86400.d0을 곱한다. 해석: 코드 입력 단위는 일이다. |
| F39-04 | IDEN=2·3·4 프로필·순서 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:19` ``(bc(j,k), k=1, :ref:`NFEN <NFEN>`)``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:32` `- If IDEN=2: bc(j,k) represents salinity boundary condition values`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:33` `- If IDEN=3: bc(j,k) represents temperature boundary condition values`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:34` `- If IDEN=4: bc(j,k) should be replaced with salbc(j,k),tempbc(j,k)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort39.rst:35` `- NFEN is the number of vertical levels in the model` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:968` `DO J=1,totalbcrivernodes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:970` `READ(39,*) (BCRivSalN1(J,K),K=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:972` `READ(39,*) (BCRivTempN1(J,K),K=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:974` `READ(39,*) (BCRivSalN1(J,K), BCRivTempN1(J,K),K=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:977` `DO J=1,totalbcrivernodes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:979` `READ(39,*) (BCRivSalN2(J,K),K=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:981` `READ(39,*) (BCRivTempN2(J,K),K=1,NFEN)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:983` `READ(39,*) (BCRivSalN2(J,K), BCRivTempN2(J,K),K=1,NFEN)` | 일치 | 각 river 노드에서 NFEN개 프로필을 읽는다. IDEN=4는 같은 수직 레벨의 염분과 온도를 번갈아 읽는다. 두 시간 프레임을 읽는다. |
| F14-30 | IBTYPE=102·112·122: 읽기와 할당의 차이 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:113` `= 102, 112, or 122 flux specified baroclinic. In order to designate a river boundary as baroclinic, 100 should be added to the IBTYPE that would be appropriate in the barotropic case. For example, to convert a barotropic river boundary (IBTYPE of 22) to a baroclinic river boundary with freshwater inflow, change the IBTYPE to 122. If there is a 1 in the 100s place of the IBTYPE, ADCIRC will then try to read an input file (fort.39) for the salinity and/or temperature boundary condition. The format of the fort.39 file depends on the value of IDEN; see the documentation of the fort.39 file for more details.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:446` `case(0,1,2,10,11,12,20,21,22,30,32,52,102,112,122,152)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1405` `case(102,112,122,152)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/boundaries.F:353` `case(0,1,2,10,11,12,20,21,22,30,32,52,94,122)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/boundaries.F:354` `allocate(simpleFluxBoundaries(sfCount)%nodes(nvell(i)))` | 불일치 | mesh 읽기 분기는 102·112·122를 갖는다. simpleFluxBoundaries 할당 분기는 이 셋 중 122만 갖는다. 해석: 102·112 지원을 읽기 분기만으로 확정하면 안 된다. 모델을 실행하지 않았다. |

### 2.9 fort.141: 시간가변 수심

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| BATH-01 | NDDT 설정 위치·사용 조건 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:6` ```The ADCIRC Time Varying Bathymetry Input File (``fort.141``) is used by ADCIRC whenever the :ref:`NDDT <NDDT>` value in the ``fort.14`` is nonzero. There are two variations of this file, depending on the value of :ref:`NDDT <NDDT>`.```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:43` ``- This file is only required when :ref:`NDDT <NDDT>` is nonzero`` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:323` `NAMELIST /timebathycontrol/ NDDT,BTIMINC,BCHGTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:422` `READ(UNIT = 15,NML = TimeBathyControl,IOSTAT = IOS_NDDT, IOMSG=ios_nml_error_msg)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2380` `IF ( (ABS(NDDT).NE.0).AND.(abs(NDDT).NE.1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2381` `&                     .AND.(abs(NDDT).NE.2) ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:122` `IF (NDDT.NE.0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:134` `CALL openFileForRead(141,TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.141',IOS)` | 불일치 | rst는 fort.14의 NDDT를 적는다. 코드는 fort.15의 TimeBathyControl namelist에서 NDDT를 읽는다. 0·±1·±2를 허용한다. |
| BATH-02 | NDDT=±1: j·depth, NP개 레코드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:11` ``If :ref:`NDDT <NDDT>` is +/-1, then each bathymetry dataset in the file covers the full domain, and the file format is as follows:``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:17` `j, depth(j)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:24` ```- depth(j) has the same meaning as DP in the mesh file (``fort.14``)```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:45` `- Each dataset must cover all nodes in the domain`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:46` `- The number of records per dataset is fixed (equal to NP)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:125` `DP1(:) = DP(:)  !set dp1 to initial depth from fort.14 grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:126` `DP2(:) = DP(:)  !set dp2 to the same values`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:148` `IF (ABS(NDDT).EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:149` `DO I=1,NP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:150` `READ(141,*) IJK,DP2(IJK)` | 일치 | 코드는 NP개 레코드를 읽는다. IJK를 직접 배열 인덱스로 쓴다. 노드 라벨 사전을 사용하지 않는다. 수심의 부호와 단위는 fort.14 DP를 따른다. |
| BATH-03 | NDDT=±2: # 구분자 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:29` ```If :ref:`NDDT <NDDT>` is +/-2, then each dataset in the ``fort.141`` file covers only part of the domain, and the file format is as follows:```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:34` `"#"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:36` `j, depth(j)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:48` `- Each dataset can cover a different subset of nodes`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:49` `- The separation between datasets is achieved by placing a hash mark ("#") in the second column of a line` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1676` `READ(lun,'(A80)') CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1677` `IF(CDUM80(2:2).NE.'#') EXIT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1682` `READ(lun,'(A80)',end=172) CDUM80`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1683` `IF(CDUM80(2:2).EQ.'#') EXIT` | 일치 | 코드는 둘째 열의 #로 데이터셋을 나눈다. 첫째 열 #만으로 구분하지 않는다. |
| BATH-04 | NDDT=±2: 고정 형식·-99999 sentinel | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:36` `j, depth(j)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:156` `CALL NDDT2GET(141,DP2(:),-99999.d0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1680` `READ(CDUM80,'(I8,E13.5)') IJK,tmpval`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/globalio.F:1681` `if (tmpval.ne.defval) RVAL(IJK) = tmpval  !this allows us to exclude values included for record separation purposes in the parallel version` | 코드에만 | 코드는 (I8,E13.5)로 읽는다. 값이 -99999.d0이면 기존 값을 유지한다. 문서 형식은 이 sentinel과 열 폭을 적지 않는다. |
| BATH-05 | 시작 프레임·생략 노드 유지·부호별 시간 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/time_varying_bathymetry.rst:48` `- Each dataset can cover a different subset of nodes` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:125` `DP1(:) = DP(:)  !set dp1 to initial depth from fort.14 grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:126` `DP2(:) = DP(:)  !set dp2 to the same values`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:128` `BTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:129` `BTIME2=BTIME2+BTIMINC  !new ending time for this record`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2398` `&    /,9X,'THE UNIT 141 FILE BEGINS AT TIME=STATIM+BTIMINC.',`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2409` `&    /,9X,'THE UNIT 141 FILE BEGINS AT THE TIME OF THE HOT START',`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2421` `&    /,9X,'THE UNIT 141 FILE BEGINS AT TIME=STATIM+BTIMINC.',`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2432` `&    /,9X,'THE UNIT 141 FILE BEGINS AT THE TIME OF THE HOT START',` | 코드에만 | 코드는 첫 프레임을 fort.14 수심으로 채운다. 양수 NDDT는 STATIM+BTIMINC를 첫 입력 시각으로 설명한다. 음수 NDDT는 hot start 시각을 기준으로 설명한다. 생략 노드는 현재 수심을 유지한다. |

| SCH-01 | nSections·각 section | `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:406` `The first line of the schedule file specifies the parameter nSections, the number of periods in the schedule where weir height should change. Each subsequent line specifies one section, which includes the starting and ending time of the period, and ZF, the weir height at the end of the period. All weir heights are interpolated linearly in time during the change in height. The entire schedule file applies to all weir nodes that reference the schedule file from within the Time Varying Weirs input file.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:408` `Use some combination of the following time parameters on each line to specify the start and end of the weir height change period, relative to the start of the schedule.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1340` `read (99, *) MYSCHED%NSECTIONS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1341` `allocate (MYSCHED%SECTION(1:MYSCHED%NSECTIONS))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1344` `do I = 1, MYSCHED%NSECTIONS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1345` `read (99, '(A)') OrigLine`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1346` `write (modLine, '(3A)') "&Schedule ", &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1347` `trim(OrigLine), " /"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1366` `read (97, NML=SCHEDULE)` | 일치 | v53 문서의 section 개수와 이름 있는 시간·높이 레코드를 v56.2.1도 읽는다. |

Generalized values are available to make it more convenient to apply a single schedule file to many weir nodes. These shortcut values are as follows`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:422` `**-99990** – Make the final weir height equal to the mesh bathy/topo elevation at that node`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:423` `**-99991** – Add a specified amount (specified elsewhere on this line as Delta) to the weir height`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:424` `**-99992** – Subtract a specified amount (specified elsewhere on this line as Delta) from the weir height`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:425` `–**99993** – Make the final weir height equal to the original weir height as specified in the fort.14 file (useful for ending a schedule file that will be repeated).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/schedule-file/index.md:427` `**Delta** – Add/Subtract this value from the current elevation of the weir when using -99991 or -99992 for ZF.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1389` `MYSCHED%SECTION(I)%BARHT_FINAL = ZF`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1390` `MYSCHED%SECTION(I)%BARHT_DELTA = DELTA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1397` `if (ISNULL(ZF)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1401` `elseif ((abs(ZF - (-99991d0)) < eps) .or. &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1402` `(abs(ZF - (-99992d0)) < eps)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1403` `if (ISNULL(DELTA)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:821` `case (-99990)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:825` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = -DP(NNBB1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:828` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:829` `min(-DP(NNBB1), -DP(NNBB2))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:831` `case (-99991)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:835` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:836` `BARLANHT2(MYIDX) + &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:837` `BAR_SCHEDULE(MYIDX)% &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:838` `SECTION(MYSEC)%BARHT_DELTA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:846` `case (-99992)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:850` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:851` `BARLANHT2(MYIDX) - &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:852` `BAR_SCHEDULE(MYIDX)% &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:853` `SECTION(MYSEC)%BARHT_DELTA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:861` `case (-99993)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:865` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = BARLANHT(MYIDX)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:867` `BARHT_FINAL(MYIDX) = BARINHT(MYIDX)` | 일치 | v53 문서의 -99990·-99991·-99992·-99993 기능을 v56.2.1도 갖는다. -99990의 내부 경계 값은 두 지반 높이의 최솟값으로 계산한 뒤 높이 하한 처리를 받는다. 상대 변화에는 Delta를 요구한다. |
| SCH-05 | section별 HOT | v53 schedule 전용 문서는 HOT 필드를 적지 않는다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1331` `TimeEndMin, TimeEndSec, ZF, DELTA, HOT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1363` `HOT = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1367` `if (HOT == 1) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1369` `case (17, 67, 68, 367, 368, 567, 568)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1370` `HOTSEC = DTDP*dble(ITHS)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/weir_boundary.F90:1375` `HOTSEC = 0d0` | 코드에만 | section별 HOT=1은 hot start 시각을 더한다. 기본값은 0이다. |

### 2.11 fort.rotm

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ROT-01 | 파일명·유형 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:10` `The fort.rotm file is used to rotate the geographical`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:21` `File format is short but there are three variants. General format is: first line`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:22` `is a string indicating format type, following lines are the values.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4369` `FNAME = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.rotm' ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4371` `call openFileForRead( FUNIT, FNAME, errorIO) ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4374` `read( FUNIT, *) inputtype ;` | 일치 | GBLINPUTDIR의 fort.rotm에서 유형 문자열을 읽는다. |
| ROT-02 | znorth_in_spherical_coors: lon0·lat0·주석 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:24` `#. znorth_in_spherical_coors`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:26` `lon0 lat0 ! Comment: lon0 lat0 is the center of the new north pole` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4382` `CASE ("znorth_in_spherical_coors")`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4383` `READ(FUNIT, *) PHIN, THETAN, IGNOREMSG ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4393` `CALL DRV_ROTMAT_ZNVEC( PHIN, THETAN ) ;` | 일치 | 코드는 새 북극 좌표와 IGNOREMSG를 읽는다. |
| ROT-03 | z-x-z: alpha·beta·gamma·주석 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:28` `#. z-x-z`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:30` `alpha beta gamma ! Comment: intrinsic rotation of alpha, beta, gamma` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4394` `CASE ("z-x-z")`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4395` `READ(FUNIT, *) AL, BE, GAM, IGNOREMSG ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4403` `CALL DRV_ROTMAT_ZXZROT( AL, BE, GAM ) ;` | 일치 | 코드는 세 회전값과 IGNOREMSG를 읽는다. |
| ROT-04 | rotation_matrix: 3×3 행렬 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:32` `#. rotation_matrix`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:34` `rot11 rot12 rot13`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:35` `rot21 rot22 rot23`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fortrotm.rst:36` `rot31 rot32 rot33` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4404` `CASE ("rotation_matrix")`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4405` `DO II = 1, 3`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:4406` `READ(FUNIT,*) ROTMAT(II,3) ;` | 불일치 | 코드는 각 레코드에서 값 하나만 ROTMAT(II,3)에 읽는다. 문서의 행별 세 값 읽기와 다르다. |

### 2.12 *_stat.151: 외부 관측점

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| STA-01 | elev_stat.151: 조건·개수·좌표 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst:6` ``The reading of the elev_stat.151 file is triggered when the number of elevation recording stations (:ref:`NSTAE`) in the :doc:`fort.15 <fort15>` file is set to a negative value.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst:13` ``:ref:`NSTAE2` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst:15` ``:ref:`XEL(k) <XEL>`, :ref:`YEL(k) <YEL>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/elev_stat151.rst:21` `If the value of NSTAE2 differs from the value of NSTAE (as read from the fort.15 file) the value of NSTAE2 will be used. If there are fewer than NSTAE2 stations listed in the elev_stat.151 file, ADCIRC will stop with an error. If there are more than NSTAE2 stations listed in the elev_stat.151 file, only the first NSTAE2 of them will be used.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3690` `IF (NSTAE < 0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3698` `OPEN(unit=stat_lun,file=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'elev_stat.151',`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3700` `READ(151,*) NSTAE2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3701` `IF (ABS(NSTAE2).NE. ABS(NSTAE)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3702` `NSTAE = ABS(NSTAE2)  !RESET THE VALUE TO WHAT'S IN THE FILE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5986` `DO I=1,num_stations`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5988` `READ(stat_lun,'(A132)') STATLINE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5993` `read(LVAR(1),*) xcoord(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5994` `read(LVAR(2),*) ycoord(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5996` `read(LVAR(1),*) lon(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5997` `read(LVAR(2),*) lat(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5998` `lon(i) = lon(i)*DEG2RAD`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5999` `lat(i) = lat(i)*DEG2RAD` | 일치 | 음수 관측점 수에서 외부 파일을 읽는다. 외부 개수의 절댓값으로 반복 수를 바꾼다. | STA-05 | 세 번째 필드: 관측점 이름 | 네 전용 문서의 형식은 좌표 두 필드만 적는다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5988` `READ(stat_lun,'(A132)') STATLINE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5989` `call parse(STATLINE, LVAR)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:5990` `names(I)=LVAR(3)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 A132 레코드를 분리한다. LVAR(3)을 이름으로 사용한다. |
