### 2.14 노드 속성의 후속 사용

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NA-25 | surface_submergence_state=1의 cold start 판정 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:104` `**Values**: If set to 1, the node is categorized as dry at the cold start of the simulation. If set to zero, the node is categorized as wet or dry depending on whether its depth is below or above the geoid.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:1233` `if ((loadStartDry.eqv..true.).and.(startDry(i).eq.1)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:1234` `nnodecode(i) = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:1361` `if ((loadStartDry.eqv..true.).and.(startDry(i).eq.1)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/cstart.F:1362` `nnodecode(i) = 0` | 일치 | 코드는 선택한 속성값이 정확히 1이면 nnodecode를 0으로 만든다. |
| NA-26 | surface_canopy_coefficient의 바람 차폐 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:149` `**Values**: Zero if the wind stress should be zero because of a canopy. One otherwise.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3002` `WindX = vcanopy(NodeNumber)*WindX`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:3003` `WindY = vcanopy(NodeNumber)*WindY`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:979` `ENDIF`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:980` `IF (LoadCanopyCoef)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:981` `&         CALL ApplyCanopyCoefficient(I,WindX,WindY)` | 일치 | 코드는 두 풍속 성분에 계수를 곱한다. 0은 풍속을 0으로 만든다. 1은 풍속을 유지한다. |
| NA-27 | bridge POAN의 의미·BDelX 변환 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:555` `Parameter that weights bridge pier drag between adjacent and centerline nodes.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:557` `= 2 if node represents a centerline node`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:559` `= 1 if node represents an adjacent node` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2101` `IF (BridgePilings(I,1).ne.0) THEN ! only for nodes w/piers`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2102` `BridgePilings(I,3) = 4.d0 *`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2103` `&              BridgePilings(I,3) / BridgePilings(I,4)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2565` `BDELX = BridgePilings(I,3)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2566` `FricBP=(H1/BDELX)*BK*(BK+5.d0*Fr*Fr-0.6d0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2567` `&           *(BALPHA+15.d0*BALPHA**4)` | 일치 | POAN은 중앙·인접 노드 가중치다. 코드는 BDelX를 4*BDelX/POAN으로 바꾼다. 이 변환은 BK가 0이 아닌 노드에 적용한다. |
| NA-28 | EVM의 Smagorinsky 사용 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:238` `**Units**: Specified by the user. ((length**2)/time).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.13_file.md:101` `Units: For standard eddy viscosity, units are specified by the user,((length**2)/time).  For Smagorinsky-type, unitless.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:828` `EVMEle=NCEle*(EVM(NM1)+EVM(NM2)+EVM(NM3))/3.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:831` `IF ((CSmag_Eh).or.(Smag_Comp_Flag)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:832` `EVMSmag=EVMEle*`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:833` `&             sqrt((LSXXGradA-LSYYGradA)*(LSXXGradA-LSYYGradA)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:834` `&                 +(LSYXGradA+LSXYGradA)*(LSYXGradA+LSXYGradA))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:837` `IF (EVMSmag .gt. SMAG_UPPER_LIM) EVMSmag = SMAG_UPPER_LIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:838` `IF (EVMSmag .lt. SMAG_LOWER_LIM) EVMSmag = SMAG_LOWER_LIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:840` `EVMEle=EVMSmag` | 불일치 | 코드는 조건을 만족하면 EVM 평균에 변형률 관련 항을 곱한다. 해석: 이 경로의 입력은 무차원 Smagorinsky 계수다. rst의 단위 설명은 이 경로를 누락한다. NA-17과 같은 단위 쟁점의 소비 근거다. |
| NA-29 | elemental_slope_limiter의 부호·활성 상태 지속 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:249` `**Description**: This nodal attribute is used to selectively limit the maximum elevation gradient that can occur across an element, thus improving numerical stability. Alternatively, it is also capable of merely logging individual elements where a specified elemental slope is exceeded at some point during the simulation. When this nodal attribute is loaded, warning messages will be written to the screen and to the fort.16 log file whenever the elevation gradient meets or exceeds the threshold value for the first time at a particular node. Once the elevation gradient is met or exceeded at a node, the elemental slope limiter remains active at that node for the remainder of the ADCIRC run. When the elemental slope limiter is active at a node, the water surface elevation at that node is reset to the average of the water surface elevations of the surrounding nodes. At the end of the run, a file called ESLNodes.63 will be written to indicate the nodes where the threshold elevation gradient was met or exceeded. Furthermore, If there is an ESLNodes.63 file in the input directory when ADCIRC starts, ADCIRC will load the existing ESLNodes.63 file along with the hotstart file so that the simulation can pick up where it left off, in terms of the elements where the slope is actively limited. When such a simulation finishes, it will overwrite the existing ESLNodes.63 file with a new one that reflects the updated state of limited elemental slopes.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort13.rst:257` `**Values**: Zero indicates that slope limiting is always active at that node, because a zero elevation gradient will always be met or exceeded; a positive value indicates the maximum gradient to be allowed at that node, at or beyond which the slope limiter is activated; a negative number indicates that the elevation gradients should be compared to the absolute value of the nodal attribute at this node. In the case of a negative number, ADCIRC will log a warning to the screen and to the fort.16 file the first time the elemental slope is exceeded, but ESLNodes.63 file will not be affected, and the elemental slope limiting will not actually occur at the node. A suggested value for this nodal attribute is 0.001.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3185` `grad_check: IF (dEta2Mag.GE.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3186` `&           ABS(elemental_slope_limiter_grad_max(NM(IE,I)))) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3194` `grad_case: if (elemental_slope_limiter_grad_max(nm(ie,i))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3195` `&               .ge.0.0d0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3204` `eslcount(nm(ie,i)) = 1 ! for output file`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3206` `elemental_slope_limiter_active(NM(IE,I)) = .true.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3210` `IF (elemental_slope_limiter_max_exceeded(NM(IE,I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3216` `elemental_slope_limiter_max_exceeded(NM(IE,I)) =`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3217` `&                      .true.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3129` `! jgf51.47: Per Rick's specification, start off by deactivating`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3130` `! any slope limitation triggered in the previous time step;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3131` `! we have a fresh start each time step in determining whether`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3132` `! to activate slope limiting at each node.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:3135` `elemental_slope_limiter_active(:) = .false.` | 불일치 | 코드는 절댓값 기준과 >=를 사용한다. 0 이상 값은 active 상태를 켠다. 음수 값은 첫 초과 로그 상태만 켠다. 문서는 활성 상태가 실행 종료까지 남는다고 적는다. 코드는 check_slopes 호출마다 active 배열을 .false.로 초기화한다. 초과 이력인 eslcount와 현재 active 상태를 구분해야 한다. ESLNodes.63 재입력은 NA-38에서 따로 판정한다. |
| NA-34 | subgrid_barrier의 첫 번째 성분 | 대상 문서에서 첫 번째 성분의 규약을 찾지 못했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1510` `SB1=subgridBarrier(NM1,1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1511` `SB2=subgridBarrier(NM2,1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1512` `SB3=subgridBarrier(NM3,1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1517` `IF(SB1.LT.DP_ORIG(NM1).AND.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1518` `&          SB2.LT.DP_ORIG(NM2).AND.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1519` `&          SB3.LT.DP_ORIG(NM3))THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1525` `DOT1 = E1*NODECODE(NM1)+SB1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1526` `DOT2 = E2*NODECODE(NM2)+SB2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1527` `DOT3 = E3*NODECODE(NM3)+SB3`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1530` `IF(DOT.GT.-1.2D0*H0)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1531` `DP(NM1) = MAX(SB1,DP(NM1)+SB1/SBDT)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1534` `SubgridBarrierOvertopping(NM1) = .TRUE.` | 코드에만 | 첫 번째 성분은 원래 수심과 비교한다. 코드는 수면값과 이 성분을 합해 월류 조건을 계산한다. 해석: fort.14 수심과 같은 부호의 길이 값이다. |
| F24-06 | fort.24.nc 우선 읽기·필드 | 대상 ASCII fort.24 문서는 netCDF 입력 스키마를 적지 않는다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6368` `filename_netcdf = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//"fort.24.nc"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6377` `INQUIRE(FILE=TRIM(filename_netcdf), EXIST=netcdf_file_found)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6381` `IF(netcdf_file_found)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6389` `CALL check_err(nf90_open(TRIM(filename_netcdf),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6391` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_dimid(ncid, "node", dimid_node))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6392` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_dimid(ncid, "num_constituents",`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6394` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_dimid(ncid, "char_len", dimid_charlen))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6400` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_varid(ncid, "constituents", varid_const))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6401` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_varid(ncid, "frequency", varid_freq))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6402` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_varid(ncid, "sal_amplitude", varid_amp))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6403` `CALL check_err(nf90_inq_varid(ncid, "sal_phase", varid_pha))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6405` `IF(n_const_sal.NE.ntif)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:6409` `call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message=scratchMessage)` | 코드에만 | ADCNETCDF 빌드에서 파일이 있으면 ASCII 파일보다 먼저 읽는다. 필수 차원은 node·num_constituents·char_len이다. 필수 변수는 constituents·frequency·sal_amplitude·sal_phase다. constituent 수는 NTIF와 같아야 한다. |

### 2.15 NWS 표의 모든 값: 분기 존재

### 2.16 fort.22: NWS=1·2·3·4·5·6·7

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MET-01 | NWS=1: JN·WSX·WSY·PRN·첫 시각 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:91` ``:ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`WSX(k) <WSX>`, :ref:`WSY(k) <WSY>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:94` `Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:101` `* The first set of met. data corresponds to TIME=STATIM+DTDP. Additional sets of met data must be provided at every time step, (WTIMINC = DTDP).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:102` `* Wind stress must be input in units of velocity squared (consistent with the units of gravity) and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in units of equivalent height of water (e.g., meters of water, feet of water that are consistent with the units of gravity).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:103` `* Stress in these units is obtained by dividing stress in units of force/area by the reference density of water.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:104` `* Pressure in these units is obtained by dividing pressure in units of force/area by the gravitational constant and the reference density of water.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1811` `IF(NWS.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1812` `OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:910` `READ(22,*) NHG,WSX2(I),WSY2(I),PR2(I) !read in`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:911` `WSX2(I)=RampMete*WSX2(I) ! apply ramp function`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:912` `WSY2(I)=RampMete*WSY2(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:915` `PRDIFF = RampMete*(PR2(I)-PRBCKGRND_MH2O)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:916` `PR2(I) = PRBCKGRND_MH2O + PRDIFF` | 일치 | 코드는 매 timestep에 NP개 응력·압력 값을 읽는다. 단위 변환 없이 램프 계수를 적용한다. JN은 NHG로 읽고 배열 배치에는 레코드 순서를 사용한다. |
| MET-02 | NWS=2: WTIMINC 레코드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:119` ``:ref:`WTIMINC <WTIMINC>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:124` `Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:133` `* Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1816` `OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1817` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1818` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP)` | 불일치 | rst 형식은 fort.22에 WTIMINC 레코드를 적는다. 문서의 설명 문장은 fort.15에 WTIMINC를 지정하도록 적는다. 코드는 fort.22에서 WTIMINC를 읽지 않는다. |
| MET-03 | NWS=±2: JN·WSX·WSY·PRN·시간 기준 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:121` ``:ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`WSX(k) <WSX>`, :ref:`WSY(k) <WSY>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:130` `* The units for pressure are meters of H₂O.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:131` `* If NWS = 2 or 102, the first set of met. data corresponds to TIME=STATIM.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:132` `* If NWS = -2 or -102, the first set of met data corresponds to TIME=HOT START TIME.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:134` `* Met data is interpolated in time to the ADCIRC time step.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:135` `* Wind stress must be input in units of velocity squared (consistent with the units of gravity).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:136` `* Surface atmospheric pressure must be input in units of equivalent height of water (e.g., meters of water, feet of water that are consistent with the units of gravity).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1817` `READ(22,*) + WTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2170` `IF(NWS.EQ.2) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2172` `WTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2203` `IF(NWS.EQ.-2) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2205` `WTIME1 = TimeLoc`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2207` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2208` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2191` `WINDX = WVNX1(I) + WTRATIO*(WVNX2(I)-WVNX1(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2192` `WINDY = WVNY1(I) + WTRATIO*(WVNY2(I)-WVNY1(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2193` `WSX2(I) = RampMete2*WINDX`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2194` `WSY2(I) = RampMete2*WINDY`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2197` `PRDIFF = RampMete2*((PRN1(I)+WTRatio*(PRN2(I)-PRN1(I)))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2198` `&                          -PRBCKGRND_MH2O)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2199` `PR2(I) = PRBCKGRND_MH2O + PRDIFF` | 일치 | 코드는 NP개 레코드 순서로 읽는다. 양수는 cold start 기준이다. 음수는 hot start 기준이다. 시간 보간 뒤 응력을 직접 사용한다. 압력은 물 높이 단위로 저장한다. |
| MET-04 | NWS=3: IWTIME·WSPEED·WDIR | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:153` ``:ref:`IWTIME <IWTIME>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:156` ``:ref:`WSPEED(k,j) <WSPEED>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:161` ``:ref:`WDIR(k,j) <WDIR>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:175` `* Wind velocity (@ 10 m above the water surface) must be input in units of m/s (regardless of the units of gravity).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3302` `READ(22,*) IWTIME`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3303` `IWYR = IWTIME/1000000`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3304` `IWMO = IWTIME/10000 - IWYR*100`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3305` `IWDAY = IWTIME/100 - IWYR*10000 - IWMO*100`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3306` `IWHR = IWTIME - IWYR*1000000 - IWMO*10000 - IWDAY*100`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3307` `WTIMED = TIMECONV(IWYR,IWMO,IWDAY,IWHR,0,0.0D0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3310` `READ(22,*) (WVXFN(I,J),J=1,NWLON)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3313` `READ(22,*) (WVYFN(I,J),J=1,NWLON)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3318` `WSPEED=WVXFN(I,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3319` `WDIR=WVYFN(I,J)*DEG2RAD`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3320` `WVXFN(I,J)=-WSPEED*SIN(WDIR)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3321` `WVYFN(I,J)=-WSPEED*COS(WDIR)` | 일치 | 코드는 날짜·시각과 두 격자 배열을 읽는다. 풍향의 도를 라디안으로 바꾼다. 인용한 식으로 두 풍속 성분을 만든다. |
| MET-05 | NWS=3의 좌표계 의무 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:170` `* Meteorological data is input to a longitude, latitude grid and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be in lon, lat coordinates.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3326` `IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3327` `YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3328` `XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3330` `IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3331` `YCOOR=Y(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3332` `XCOOR=X(I)` | 불일치 | 문서는 ADCIRC 격자가 lon·lat이어야 한다고 적는다. 코드는 ICS=1에서 Cartesian X·Y를 사용한 별도 보간 경로도 갖는다. 이 차이는 입력 허용 경로의 차이다. |
| MET-06 | NWS=±4: JN·WVNX·WVNY·#·생략 노드·시간 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:196` ``:ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`WVNX(k) <WVNX>`, :ref:`WVNY(k) <WVNY>`, :ref:`PRN(k) <PRN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:209` `* Meteorological data is input directly to a subset of nodes in the ADCIRC grid (as specified by the node number JN). The ADCIRC grid must be either in lon, lat coordinates or in meter-based Cartesian coordinates.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:210` `* If NWS = 4 or 104, the first set of met. data corresponds to TIME=STATIM.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:211` `* If NWS = -4 or -104, the first set of met data corresponds to TIME=HOT START TIME.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:214` `* Each data line must have the format I8, 3E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:215` `* A line containing the # symbol in column 2 indicates met data at the next time increment begins on the following line.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:216` `* At each new time, any node that is not specified in the input file is assumed to have zero wind velocity and pressure = 1013.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:219` `* WIND_VEL{m/s @ 10m} = WIND_VEL{knots @ bl average}*0.5144 (In prior ADCIRC versions, an additional factor of 1.04 was included in the formulation to convert from 30 minute avg winds to 10 minute avg winds. This factor was removed and it is currently assumed that the input wind data uses a 10 minute averaging period. Note, this is unrelated to the value of WTIMINC).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3391` `WVNX2(I)=0.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3392` `WVNY2(I)=0.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3393` `PRN2(I)=101300.d0/RHOWATG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3396` `READ(22,'(A80)') PBLJAGF`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3397` `IF(PBLJAGF(2:2).NE.'#') EXIT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3400` `171     READ(PBLJAGF,'(I8,5E13.5)') NHG,WVNX2(NHG),WVNY2(NHG),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3401` `&                               PRN2(NHG)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3409` `WVNX2(NHG)=WVNX2(NHG)*0.5144d0 !CONVERT KNOTS TO  M/S`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3410` `WVNY2(NHG)=WVNY2(NHG)*0.5144d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3412` `IF(PRN2(NHG).EQ.0.) PRN2(NHG)=101300.d0/RHOWATG                       !RAL0315+ OK`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3414` `IF(PBLJAGF(2:2).EQ.'#') EXIT` | 일치 | 코드는 고정 형식으로 지정 노드에 읽는다. 둘째 열 #로 데이터셋을 나눈다. 생략 노드는 풍속 0과 1013 mbar다. knots에 0.5144를 곱한다. 고정 형식의 남는 edit descriptor는 추가 입력 필드를 뜻하지 않는다. |
| MET-07 | NWS=±4: PRN의 단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:217` `* Wind velocity (assumed to be 10m 10 minute averaged value) must be input in knots and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in hundredths of a millibar.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:224` `* The following relationship is used in ADCIRC to convert to pressure in meters of water from pressure in hundredths of a millibar`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:225` `* PRESSURE{m H2O}=PRESSURE{Pa/100}*100/(GRAVITY*DENSITY H2O).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3411` `PRN2(NHG)=100.d0*PRN2(NHG)/RHOWATG !CONVERT MILLIBARS TO M OF WATER`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3412` `IF(PRN2(NHG).EQ.0.) PRN2(NHG)=101300.d0/RHOWATG                       !RAL0315+ OK` | 불일치 | 문서는 hundredths of a millibar를 적는다. 코드 주석은 MILLIBARS를 적는다. 코드는 입력값에 100을 곱한 뒤 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 해석: 이 변환이 요구하는 입력은 mbar다. |
| MET-08 | NWS=±5: JN·WVX·WVY·PRN·시간 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:241` ``:ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`WVX(k) <WVX>`, :ref:`WVY(k) <WVY>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:249` `* Meteorological data is input directly to all nodes in the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be either in lon, lat coordinates or in meter-based Cartesian coordinates.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:250` `* If NWS = 5 or 105, the first set of met. data corresponds to TIME=STATIM.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:251` `* If NWS = -5 or -105, the first set of met data corresponds to TIME=HOT START TIME.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:252` `* Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:254` `* Wind velocity (@ 10 m above the water surface) must be input in m/s and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in meters of water.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1867` `IF(ABS(NWS).EQ.5) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1868` `OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1869` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1870` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1871` `WTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1872` `WTIME2 = WTIME1 + WTIMINC` | 일치 | 코드는 모든 노드의 풍속 m/s와 물 높이 단위 압력을 읽는다. NHG를 배열 인덱스로 사용하지 않는다. 부호별 hot start 처리는 MET-39에 적었다. |
| MET-09 | NWS=6: 날짜·시각 여섯 필드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:274` `IMDAY, IMMONTH, IMYEAR, IMHOUR, IMMIN, IMSEC` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3520` `DO I=1,NWLAT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3521` `DO J=1,NWLON`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3522` `READ(22,*) WVXFN(I,J),WVYFN(I,J),PRN(I,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3523` `END DO`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3524` `END DO` | 불일치 | 코드는 첫 레코드부터 U·V·P를 읽는다. fort.22에서 IMDAY·IMMONTH·IMYEAR·IMHOUR·IMMIN·IMSEC를 읽지 않는다. |
| MET-10 | NWS=6: WVXFN·WVYFN·PRN·격자·단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:277` ``:ref:`WVXFN(k,j) <WVXFN>`, :ref:`WVYFN(k,j) <WVYFN>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:286` `* In versions 43 and earlier the format of input was P, U, V. The input has now changed to U, V, P to be consistent with other NWS formats.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:287` `* Meteorological data is input on a rectangular grid (either in Longitude, Latitude or Cartesian coordinates, consistent with the grid coordinates) and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:288` `* In setting up the meteorological grid it is assumed that y (e.g., latitude) varies from north (k=1) to south (k=NWLAT) and x (e.g., longitude) varies from west (j=1) to east (j=NWLON).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:291` `* The meteorological grid MUST cover the entire ADCIRC mesh; that is, the ADCIRC mesh must be ENTIRELY within the meteorological grid or an error will result.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:298` `* Wind velocity (@ 10 m above the water surface) must be input in units of m/s and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in units of Pascals = Newtons/square meter.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:305` `* PRESSURE{m H2O}=PRESSURE{Pascal}/(GRAVITY*DENSITY H2O).` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3522` `READ(22,*) WVXFN(I,J),WVYFN(I,J),PRN(I,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3527` `IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3528` `YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3529` `XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3531` `IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3532` `YCOOR=Y(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3533` `XCOOR=X(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3557` `PRN2(I) = PRN(LATIND2,LONIND2)*XWRATIO*YWRATIO`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3558` `&        + PRN(LATIND2,LONIND1)*(1.d0-XWRATIO)*YWRATIO`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3559` `&        + PRN(LATIND1,LONIND2)*XWRATIO*(1.d0-YWRATIO)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3560` `&        + PRN(LATIND1,LONIND1)*(1.d0-XWRATIO)*(1.d0-YWRATIO)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3561` `PRN2(I) = PRN2(I)/RHOWATG` | 일치 | 코드는 U·V·P 순서로 읽는다. 격자 보간을 수행한다. Pa 압력을 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 전체 격자 포함 검사의 원문은 MET-40에 적었다. |
| MET-11 | NWS=±7: WVXFN·WVYFN·PRN | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:310` `NWS = 7 or -7: Wind Stress and Pressure on Rectangular Grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:320` `for k=1 to NWLAT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:321` `for j=1 to NWLON`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:322` ``:ref:`WVXFN(k,j) <WVXFN>`, :ref:`WVYFN(k,j) <WVYFN>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` `` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1892` `IF(ABS(NWS).EQ.7) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3650` `DO K=1,NWLAT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3651` `DO J=1,NWLON`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3652` `READ(22,*) WVXFN(K,J),WVYFN(K,J),PRN(K,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3691` `PRN2(I) = PRN2(I)/RHOWATG` | 일치 | 격자 응력 성분과 압력을 읽는다. 압력을 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 응력의 단위와 시간 조건은 MET-41에 적었다. |

### 2.17 fort.22: NWS=8·19·20·30의 트랙 레코드

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MET-12 | NWS=8: 날짜·TECH·TAU | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:342` `AL, ##, YYYY, MM/DD/HH24, TECH, TAU, LatN/S, LongE/W, VMAX, MSLP, TY, ...`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:355` `* When reading lines labeled "BEST" from the fort.22, it obtains timing information from the year, month, day, and hour in column 3.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:356` `* When reading lines labeled "OFCL" from the fort.22, it uses the forecast increment (a.k.a. TAU) from column 6.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:944` `read (UNIT=22, FMT=228, end=9999) &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:945` `iYear(i), iMth(i), iDay(i), iHr(i), &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:946` `CastType(i), iFcstInc(i), iLat(i), ns(i), iLon(i), ew(i), &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:972` `CastTime(i) = TimeConv(iYear(i), iMth(i), iDay(i), iHr(i), 0, 0.d0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:975` `CastTime(i) = CastTime(i) - WindRefTime`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:998` `FcstInc(i) = FcstInc(i)*3600.d0 ! convert hours to seconds`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:999` `CastTime(i) = CastTime(i - 1) + (FcstInc(i) - FcstInc(i - 1))` | 일치 | 코드는 BEST의 날짜를 초로 바꾼다. 코드는 OFCL의 예보 간격에 3600을 곱한다. 중복 날짜·예보 간격 레코드를 건너뛴다. |
| MET-13 | NWS=8: LatN/S·LongE/W | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:342` `AL, ##, YYYY, MM/DD/HH24, TECH, TAU, LatN/S, LongE/W, VMAX, MSLP, TY, ...`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:353` `* The symmetric vortex model (NWS=8) in ADCIRC assumes that the longitudes in the fort.22 are west longitude, so it multiplies the longitude values by -1. It ignores the 'E' or 'W' in the longitude column of the fort.22.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:946` `CastType(i), iFcstInc(i), iLat(i), ns(i), iLon(i), ew(i), &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1047` `Lat(i) = Lat(i)/10.d0 ! convert 10ths of degs to degs`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1048` `Lon(i) = Lon(i)/10.d0 ! convert 10ths of degs to degs`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1049` `if (ew(i) == 'W') then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1050` `Lon(i) = -1.d0*Lon(i) ! negative b/c WEST longitude`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1052` `if (ns(i) == 'S') then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1053` `Lat(i) = -1.d0*Lat(i) ! negative b/c SOUTH latitude` | 불일치 | 문서는 E/W를 무시하고 서경으로 고정한다고 적는다. 코드는 W일 때 경도 부호를 바꾼다. 코드는 S일 때 위도 부호를 바꾼다. |
| MET-14 | NWS=8: VMAX·MSLP·고정 폭 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:342` `AL, ##, YYYY, MM/DD/HH24, TECH, TAU, LatN/S, LongE/W, VMAX, MSLP, TY, ...`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:349` `* The input file is fixed width (not comma separated values or csv) and is assumed to correspond to the ATCF Best Track/Objective Aid/Wind Radii Format.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:947` `iSpd(i), iCPress(i), iRRP(i), iRMW(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:950` `228      format(8x, i4, i2, i2, i2, 6x, a4, 2x, i3, 1x, i4, a1, 2x, i4, a1, 2x, i3, 2x, i4, 47x, i3, 2x, i3)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1055` `CPress(i) = CPress(i)*100.d0 ! convert mbar to Pa`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1058` `Spd(i) = Spd(i)*0.51444444d0 ! convert kts to m/s` | 일치 | 코드는 고정 폭 정수 필드를 읽는다. 코드는 기압 mbar를 Pa로 바꾼다. 코드는 풍속 knot를 m/s로 바꾼다. 단위 변환의 수치는 코드 근거이다. |
| MET-15 | NWS=8: RRP·RMW·CALM | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:342` `AL, ##, YYYY, MM/DD/HH24, TECH, TAU, LatN/S, LongE/W, VMAX, MSLP, TY, ...`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:354` `* The symmetric vortex model (NWS=8) does not use any of the isotach wind speeds or wind radii data.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:355` `* When reading lines labeled "BEST" from the fort.22, it obtains timing information from the year, month, day, and hour in column 3.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:356` `* When reading lines labeled "OFCL" from the fort.22, it uses the forecast increment (a.k.a. TAU) from column 6.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:947` `iSpd(i), iCPress(i), iRRP(i), iRMW(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1015` `case ("CALM")`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1024` `if (iFcstInc(i) == 0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1028` `FcstInc(i) = FcstInc(i)*3600.d0 ! convert hours to seconds`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1032` `case DEFAULT ! unrecognized`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1034` `message='Only "BEST", "OFCL", or "CALM" are allowed '// &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1035` `'in the 5th column of fort.22.')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1056` `RRP(i) = RRP(i)*1.852000003180799d0*1000.0d0 ! convert nm to m`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:1057` `RMW(i) = RMW(i)*1.852000003180799d0 ! convert nm to km` | 코드에만 | 간략 형식은 RRP·RMW를 나열하지 않는다. 코드는 두 반경을 읽고 각각 m·km로 바꾼다. 문서의 TECH 설명은 BEST·OFCL만 적는다. 코드는 CALM을 추가로 허용한다. 이 행은 외부 ATCF 규격의 누락 여부를 판정하지 않는다. |
| MET-16 | NWS=8: 모델 이름 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:330` `NWS = 8 or 108: Dynamic Symmetric Holland Vortex Model`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:333` `In this format, a single tropical cyclone model using the Generalized asymmetric Holland formulation with a moving reference frame and a background wind field is used.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:531` `call HollandGet(WVNX, WVNY, PRESS, TIMELOC, FoundEye, EyeLon, EyeLat)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nws08.F90:562` `subroutine HollandGet(WVNX, WVNY, PRESS, TIMELOC, FoundEye, EyeLon, EyeLat)` | 불일치 | rst 본문은 Generalized asymmetric Holland로 설명한다. 코드의 NWS8 루틴은 HollandGet을 호출한다. GAHM은 MET-18의 NWS=20에서 처리한다. |
| MET-17 | NWS=19: 고정 폭 전체 레코드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:624` `* The format of the file is fixed and users will want to use the aswip program to be sure that the input fort.22 file is properly formatted.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5216` `26       FORMAT(3x, i3, 2x, i4, 3i2, 6x, a4, 2(2x,i3),a1, 2x, i4,a1, 2x,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5217` `&         i3,2x, i4, 6x, i3, 7x, 4(i4,2x), i4, 8x, i3,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5218` `&          27x, 2(i3,2x), a10, 2x, i4, 2x, i4,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5219` `&         2x, 4(i1,2x),2x, 4(f6.1,2x), 2x, f8.4) !,2(2x,f10.4))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5223` `READ(22,26) advr1(ient), iyear1(ient),imth1(ient),iday1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5224` `&    ihr1(ient), type1(ient),iFcstInc1(ient), ilat1(ient),ns,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5225` `&    ilon1(ient),ew, ispd1(ient), icpress1(ient), ivr1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5226` `&    (ir1(ient,i),i=1,4), ipn1(ient), atcfRMW1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5227` `&    dir1(ient),speed1(ient), name1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5228` `&    cycle_num1(ient),isotachs_per_cycle1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5229` `&    (quadflag1(ient,i),i=1,4),(rmaxw1(ient,i),i=1,4),HollB1(ient)` | 일치 | 코드는 날짜·TECH·TAU·좌표·풍속·기압·등풍속선·반경·배경 기압·RMW·이동 방향·이동 속도·이름·cycle 번호·등풍속선 수·선택 값·네 Rmax·Holland B 순서로 읽는다. |
| MET-18 | NWS=20: 고정 폭·추가 여덟 필드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:638` `The file format is similar to the NWS = 19 format with 8 additional columns of data, see notes below for more information.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:659` `* Column 39: Holland B parameter computed using the formulas outlined in the Holland paper, and implemented using the aswip program.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:660` `* Columns 40-43: Quadrant-varying Holland B parameter`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:661` `* Columns 44-47: Quadrant-varying Vmax calculated at the top of the planetary boundary (a wind reduction factor is applied to reduce the wind speed at the boundary to the 10-m surface)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:662` `* The format of the file is fixed and users will want to use the aswip program to be sure that the input fort.22 file is properly formatted.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5798` `27       FORMAT(3x, i3, 2x, i4, 3i2, 6x, a4, 2(2x,i3),a1, 2x, i4,a1, 2x,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5799` `&         i3,2x, i4, 6x, i3, 7x, 4(i4,2x), i4, 8x, i3,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5800` `&          27x, 2(i3,2x), a10, 2x, i4, 2x, i4,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5801` `&         2x, 4(i1,2x),2x, 4(f6.1,2x),1x, 8(f8.4,2x), f8.4)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5805` `READ(22,27) advr1(ient), iyear1(ient),imth1(ient),iday1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5806` `&    ihr1(ient), type1(ient),iFcstInc1(ient), ilat1(ient),ns,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5807` `&    ilon1(ient),ew, ispd1(ient), icpress1(ient), ivr1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5808` `&    (ir1(ient,i),i=1,4), ipn1(ient), atcfRMW1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5809` `&    dir1(ient),speed1(ient), name1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5810` `&    cycle_num1(ient),isotachs_per_cycle1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5811` `&    (quadflag1(ient,i),i=1,4),(rmaxw1(ient,i),i=1,4),HollB1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5812` `&    (HollBs1(ient,i),i=1,4),(VmaxesBL1(ient,i),i=1,4)` | 일치 | 코드는 NWS=19의 필드 뒤에서 HollBs 네 값과 VmaxesBL 네 값을 읽는다. 조건과 필드 순서는 문서와 같다. |
| MET-19 | NWS=19·20: TAU | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:609` `* Column 6: Forecast time in hours (enter the time in hours in each record starting at 0)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5258` `CycleTime(icyc) =  iFcstInc1(ient) * 3600.d0 + timeOffset` | 일치 | 코드는 예보 시간을 시간 단위로 읽고 초로 바꾼다. NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |
| MET-20 | NWS=19·20: Latitude·Longitude | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:610` `* Column 7: Latitude of the eye`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:611` `* Column 8: Longitude of the eye` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5224` `&    ihr1(ient), type1(ient),iFcstInc1(ient), ilat1(ient),ns,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5225` `&    ilon1(ient),ew, ispd1(ient), icpress1(ient), ivr1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5262` `IF(ew.EQ.'E')THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5263` `ilon(icyc)=(-1)*ilon(icyc)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5265` `IF(ns.EQ.'S')THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5266` `ilat(icyc)=(-1)*ilat(icyc)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5268` `lon(icyc) = (-1.0d0) * ilon(icyc) * 0.1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5269` `lat(icyc)=ilat(icyc) * 0.1d0` | 일치 | 코드는 위도·경도의 0.1도 정수와 N/S·E/W 필드를 읽는다. 코드는 남위·서경의 부호를 반영한다. NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |
| MET-21 | NWS=19·20: VMAX·MSLP | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:612` `* Column 9: Maximum sustained wind speed in knots`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:613` `* Column 10: Minimum sea level pressure in MB` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5225` `&    ilon1(ient),ew, ispd1(ient), icpress1(ient), ivr1(ient),` | 일치 | 코드는 최대 풍속과 중심 기압을 읽는다. NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |

NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |

NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |
| MET-24 | NWS=19·20: 이름·cycle 번호·등풍속선 수 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:618` `* Column 28: Storm Name in ATCF file format`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:619` `* Column 29: Time Record number. There can be multiple lines for a given time record depending on the number of isotachs reported in the ATCF File`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:620` `* Column 30: Number of isotachs reported in the ATCF file for the corresponding Time record.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5227` `&    dir1(ient),speed1(ient), name1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5228` `&    cycle_num1(ient),isotachs_per_cycle1(ient),` | 일치 | 코드는 이름과 두 개의 레코드 계수 필드를 읽는다. | MET-25 | NWS=19·20: 선택 값·네 Rmax·Holland B | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:621` `* Columns 31-34: Selection of radii for that particular isotach. 0 indicates do not use this radius, and 1 indicates use this radius and corresponding wind speed.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:622` `* Columns 35-38: Designated Rmax values computed for each of the quadrants selected for each particular isotach.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:623` `* Column 39: Holland B parameter computed using the formulas outlined in the Holland paper, and implemented using the aswip program.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5229` `&    (quadflag1(ient,i),i=1,4),(rmaxw1(ient,i),i=1,4),HollB1(ient)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5273` `if (quadflag1(ient,i)==1) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5274` `rmaxw(icyc,i) = rmaxw1(ient,i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5275` `quadflag(icyc,i) = quadflag1(ient,i)` | 일치 | 코드는 quadflag가 1인 사분면의 Rmax를 채택한다. NWS=20의 동일 필드 읽기는 MET-18에 인용했다. |
| MET-26 | NWS=19·20: 이동 방향·이동 속도·추가 식별 필드 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:608` `* The NWS=19 option requires the following variables in the fort.22 file in a best track format`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:644` `* The NWS=20 option requires the following variables in the fort.22 file in a best track format, which includes the same variables as NWS=19 plus additional columns:` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5223` `READ(22,26) advr1(ient), iyear1(ient),imth1(ient),iday1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5224` `&    ihr1(ient), type1(ient),iFcstInc1(ient), ilat1(ient),ns,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5227` `&    dir1(ient),speed1(ient), name1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5255` `uTrans(icyc) = sin(dir1(ient)*deg2rad)*speed1(ient)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5256` `vTrans(icyc) = cos(dir1(ient)*deg2rad)*speed1(ient)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5805` `READ(22,27) advr1(ient), iyear1(ient),imth1(ient),iday1(ient),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5806` `&    ihr1(ient), type1(ient),iFcstInc1(ient), ilat1(ient),ns,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5809` `&    dir1(ient),speed1(ient), name1(ient),` | 코드에만 | 문서의 필수 변수 목록은 advr·날짜·TECH·dir·speed를 별도로 설명하지 않는다. 코드는 dir을 deg2rad로 바꾸고 이동 속도의 동서·남북 성분을 만든다. ATCF 외부 규격 자체는 대조 대상에 포함하지 않았다. |
| MET-27 | NWS=20: 사분면 B·경계층 Vmax의 사용·단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:660` `* Columns 40-43: Quadrant-varying Holland B parameter`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:661` `* Columns 44-47: Quadrant-varying Vmax calculated at the top of the planetary boundary (a wind reduction factor is applied to reduce the wind speed at the boundary to the 10-m surface)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5936` `if (quadflag1(ient,i)==1) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5938` `rmaxw(icyc,i,isot) = rmaxw1(ient,i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5940` `HollBs(icyc,i,isot) = HollBs1(ient,i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:5941` `VmaxesBL(icyc,i,isot) = VmaxesBL1(ient,i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6119` `&                       cVmwBL(i)*kt2ms*crmaxw(i)*nm2m*corio/`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6120` `&                       (cHollBs(i)*((cVmwBL(i)*kt2ms)**2+`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6121` `&                       cVmwBL(i)*kt2ms*crmaxw(i)*nm2m*corio))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6201` `CALL uvpr(dist(i),aziangle(i),crmaxw(i),crmaxwTrue(i),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6202` `&        cHollBs(i),cVmwBL(i),cPhiFactor(i),stormMotionU,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6203` `&        stormMotionV,geofactor,wvnx(i),wvny(i),press(i))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6216` `press(i) = press(i) / RhoWatG` | 일치 | 코드는 선택한 사분면의 HollBs·VmaxesBL을 저장한다. 코드는 풍속에 kt2ms를 적용한다. 코드는 반경에 nm2m을 적용한다. 코드는 계산한 Pa 기압을 RhoWatG로 나눈다. |
| MET-28 | NWS=20: 입력 후 기압·풍속 한계 | 현행 rst의 NWS=20 필수 변수 목록에 없음. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6207` `press(i) = max(0.85d5,min(1.1d5,press(i)))  ! Typhoon Tip 870 hPa ... 12-oct-1979`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6208` `wvnx(i)  = max(-200.d0,min(200.d0,wvnx(i)))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6209` `wvny(i)  = max(-200.d0,min(200.d0,wvny(i)))` | 코드에만 | 코드는 기압을 85000–110000 Pa로 제한한다. 코드는 각 풍속 성분을 -200–200 m/s로 제한한다. 원문 주석의 수정 날짜가 코드 판 표시보다 늦다는 사실을 판 머리말에서 구분했다. |
| MET-29 | NWS=30: 파일 이름·전이 거리 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:679` `This format is a combination of GAHM (NWS=20) and a gridded background meteorological field (NWS=12). The core of a tropical cyclone is represented using the GAHM model and read in from a file named NWS_20_fort.22, which should be created following the procedure presented in the section on NWS=20. The user must also supply gridded wind and pressure files in the form of an OWI-style fort.22 file, a fort.221 file, and a fort.222 file, details on these are in the section on NWS=12. ADCIRC internally blends the wind and pressure fields from these two sets of inputs over a user-controlled distance. Specifically, the pureVortex and pureBackground inputs in the fort.15 file (for formatting, see info on the meteorological parameter line) are coefficients used to define where transitions between the vortex and background meteorology occur. At or within the distance pureVortex*vortexRMW of the storm's center, the GAHM meteorology is used. At or beyond the distance pureBackground*vortexRMW of the storm's center, the background meteorology is used. In between these, a linear distance-weighted average of the two sets of meteorology is used to define the forcing.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1928` `IF ((ABS(NWS).EQ.12).OR.(ABS(NWS).EQ.29).OR.(ABS(NWS).EQ.30)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1929` `CALL NWS12INIT(WVNX1,WVNY1,PRN1,NP,PRBCKGRND)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1945` `IF ((abs(NWS).EQ.29).or.(abs(NWS).eq.30)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1953` `if (abs(nws).eq.30) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1954` `OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'NWS_20_fort.22',`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1956` `CALL NWS20GET(vortexWVNX2,vortexWVNY2,vortexPRN2,STATIM*86400.D0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:830` `vortexOnly = vortex_distance * vortex_rmw`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:831` `&           * 1.852000003180799d0 * 1000.0d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:832` `backgroundOnly = background_distance * vortex_rmw`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:833` `&           * 1.852000003180799d0 * 1000.0d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:835` `IF ( rad.le.vortexOnly ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:836` `bf = 1.0d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:837` `ELSEIF ( rad.ge.backgroundOnly )THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:838` `bf = 0.0d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:841` `bf = (backgroundOnly-rad)/(backgroundOnly-vortexOnly)` | 일치 | 코드는 OWI 배경 입력과 NWS_20_fort.22를 읽는다. 코드는 RMW 배수로 전이 거리를 만든다. 코드는 두 거리 사이에 선형 가중치를 사용한다. |
| MET-30 | NWS=30: 결합하는 변수 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:679` `This format is a combination of GAHM (NWS=20) and a gridded background meteorological field (NWS=12). The core of a tropical cyclone is represented using the GAHM model and read in from a file named NWS_20_fort.22, which should be created following the procedure presented in the section on NWS=20. The user must also supply gridded wind and pressure files in the form of an OWI-style fort.22 file, a fort.221 file, and a fort.222 file, details on these are in the section on NWS=12. ADCIRC internally blends the wind and pressure fields from these two sets of inputs over a user-controlled distance. Specifically, the pureVortex and pureBackground inputs in the fort.15 file (for formatting, see info on the meteorological parameter line) are coefficients used to define where transitions between the vortex and background meteorology occur. At or within the distance pureVortex*vortexRMW of the storm's center, the GAHM meteorology is used. At or beyond the distance pureBackground*vortexRMW of the storm's center, the background meteorology is used. In between these, a linear distance-weighted average of the two sets of meteorology is used to define the forcing.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1589` `! blend the wind stresses and barometric pressures`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1590` `WSX2(I) = bf*RampMete*airwaterdensityrat*WDragCo*WindX*WindMag`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1591` `&               +(1.0d0-bf)*WSX2(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1592` `WSY2(I) = bf*RampMete*airwaterdensityrat*WDragCo*WindY*WindMag`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1593` `&               +(1.0d0-bf)*WSY2(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1596` `PR2(I)= bf*vortexPRN2(I)+(1.0d0-bf)*PR2(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1597` `WVNXOUT(I)=bf*RampMete*WindX+(1.0d0-bf)*WVNXOUT(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1598` `WVNYOUT(I)=bf*RampMete*WindY+(1.0d0-bf)*WVNYOUT(I)` | 코드에만 | 코드는 운동량 forcing에 바람 응력을 결합한다. 코드는 출력 풍속과 기압도 결합한다. 해석: 문서의 meteorology라는 표현만으로 풍속 결합 후 응력 계산을 요구한다고 판정할 수 없다. |
| MET-31 | aswip의 -n·-m·-z 및 추가 입력 옵션 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:625` ```* The command line for NWS=19 is ``./aswip -n 19 -m 2 -z 1`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:664` ```* ``./aswip -n # -m # -z #`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:665` `* -n = nws option`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:666` `* -m = methods of selecting isotachs for use in computation of radius/radii to maximum winds`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:667` `* 1: always use the 34kt isotach`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:668` `* 2: use the highest available isotach in any quadrant each time`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:669` `* 3: use the 50kt isotach if it is available; otherwise use the 34kt isotach`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:670` `* 4: use all available isotachs (must choose this for GAHM/NWS=20)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:671` `* -z = approaches solving for Rmax, 1 = only rotate wind vectors afterward, 2 = rotate wind vectors before and afterwards (use this for NWS=20)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:672` ```* The command line for NWS=20 is: ``./aswip -n 20 -m 4 -z 2`` ``` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2902` `CASE("-M","-m")  ! method of selecting quadrant Rmax values`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2905` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) method`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2906` `CASE("-Z","-z")  ! approach of calculating quadrantVr`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2909` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) approach`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2915` `CASE("-N","-n")  ! nws parameter (19, or 20)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2918` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) nws`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2892` `CASE("-I","-i")  ! input fort.14 file containing mesh locations`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2895` `meshFileName = trim(CMDLINEARG)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2897` `CASE("-L","-l")  ! input text file containing station locations`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2900` `meshFileName = trim(CMDLINEARG)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2910` `CASE("-O","-o")  ! time increment for writing output (seconds)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2914` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) output_increment`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2919` `CASE("-P","-p")  ! percent change of rmax relative to calculated val`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2922` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) rmax_percent`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2926` `CASE("-X","-x")  ! percent change of vmax relative to given val`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2929` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) vmax_percent`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2930` `CASE("-T","-t")  ! time offset (like hotstart time)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2933` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) timeOffset`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2935` `CASE("-R","-r")  ! constant forecastRmax`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2938` `READ(CMDLINEARG,*) forecastRmax`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2945` `CASE("-W","-w") ! the name of the track file to read`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/wind/aswip.F:2950` `infile = trim(CMDLINEARG)` | 일치 | 코드는 -n·-m·-z와 각 대문자 별칭의 인자를 읽는다. 추가 옵션의 문서 부재 목록은 3절에 적는다. 이 행은 isotach 선택 알고리즘 내부의 완전성을 판정하지 않는다. |

degrees`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6737` `do i=1,4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6739` `read(220,'(A)',end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) scratchMessage`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6742` `read(220,*,end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) hWindFiles(cn)%nh`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6765` `indexLimit = (hWindFiles(cn)%nh - 1) / 2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6774` `do j=-indexLimit,indexLimit`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6776` `read(220,'(2(A1,F13.5,A1,F13.5,A1))',end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6777` `&       (paren, hWindFiles(cn)%u(i,j), comma,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6778` `&        hWindFiles(cn)%v(i,j), paren,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6779` `&        i=-indexLimit, indexLimit)` | 일치 | 코드는 중심 경도·위도를 도 단위로 읽는다. 코드는 각 snapshot의 nh를 읽고 정사각 배열을 만든다. 코드는 괄호·쉼표를 포함한 풍속 성분을 읽는다. 별도 기압 필드를 읽지 않는다. |

### 2.19 fort.200 계열: NWS=10·11

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| F200-01 | NWS=10: 격자 크기·P/U/V 레코드·단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:17` ``for k=1, :ref:`LONB <LONB>` * :ref:`LATB <LATB>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:18` ``:ref:`PG(k) <PG>`, :ref:`UG(k) <UG>`, :ref:`VG(k) <VG>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:35` `- Meteorological data is input to a T1534, Gaussian grid (NWLON*NWLAT = 3072*1536 = 4,718,592)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:48` `- Wind velocity (@ 10m): m/s`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:49` `- Surface pressure: Pascals (N/m²)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:66` `PRESSURE{m H2O} = PRESSURE{Pascal}/(GRAVITY*DENSITY of H2O)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:221` `integer, parameter :: latb = 1536 !190 change to gfs t1534 grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:222` `integer, parameter :: lonb = 3072 !384 change to gfs t1534 grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3953` `read(iext,*) pg(i),ug(i),vg(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3976` `pll(n)=p1*d00(n)+p2*d10(n)+p3*d11(n)+p4*d01(n)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3977` `ull(n)=u1*d00(n)+u2*d10(n)+u3*d11(n)+u4*d01(n)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3978` `vll(n)=v1*d00(n)+v2*d10(n)+v3*d11(n)+v4*d01(n)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3979` `pll(n)=pll(n)/rhowatg` | 일치 | 코드는 3072×1536개 격자 값을 읽는다. 코드는 Pa 기압을 rhowatg로 나눈다. 코드는 m/s 풍속을 보간한다. |
| F200-02 | NWS=10: 순차 파일 번호·WTIMINC·cold/hot start | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:38` `- Files are named sequentially: fort.200, fort.200+N, fort.200+2*N, etc.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:39` `- N = WTIMINC/3600 (time interval in hours between data files)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:40` `- WTIMINC must be evenly divisible by 3600`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:42` `- Winds are assumed at rest at TIME=STATIM`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:43` `- First data file (fort.200+N) corresponds to TIME=STATIM+WTIMINC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:45` `- Initial file (fort.200) required at TIME=HOT START TIME` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3743` `if (ihot.eq.0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3744` `nws10DatasetCounter = 1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3745` `else`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3746` `nws10DatasetCounter = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3927` `if (modulo(int(wtiminc),3600).ne.0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3928` `call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3933` `iext = 200 + nws10DatasetCounter * int(wtiminc/3600.d0)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3934` `write(fname1,'("fort.",i3)') iext`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3938` `call openFileForRead(iext,trim(gblinputdir)//'/'//trim(fname1),ios)` | 일치 | 코드는 WTIMINC의 정수 부분이 3600으로 나누어떨어지는지 검사한다. cold start는 counter=1로 시작한다. hot start는 counter=0으로 시작한다. |
| F200-03 | NWS=10: 선택적인 얼음 열 IG | fort200.rst의 NWS=10 레코드는 PG·UG·VG만 나열한다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1217` `IF (NCICE.eq.10) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1219` `call nws10get(wvnx2,wvny2,prn2,cice2)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3946` `if (present(ill)) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3948` `read(iext,*) pg(i),ug(i),vg(i),ig(i)` | 코드에만 | NCICE=10이면 코드는 네 번째 얼음 필드를 읽는다. present(ill)이 데이터 읽기 목록을 바꾼다. |

- VE29*SBETAU`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4226` `WVNY2(N)=UE29*SBETAU + VE29*CBETAU` | 일치 | 코드는 181×271개 배열을 사용한다. 코드는 파일 번호에 NWSEGWI를 더한다. 코드는 날짜 네 필드를 읽는다. 코드는 E-grid 풍속 성분을 | F200-06 | NWS=11: binary OPEN의 FORM | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:81` `- File type: binary (ACCESS='sequential', FORM='unformatted')` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4187` `call openFileForRead(IEXT,FNAME1,errorIO)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4195` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) IYEAR,IMONTH,IDAY,IHOUR`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4196` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/io.F90:100` `open (lun, file=trim(filename), status='OLD', &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/io.F90:101` `action='READ', iostat=errorIO)` | 불일치 | 문서는 FORM='unformatted'를 요구한다. 실제 호출한 openFileForRead는 FORM을 지정하지 않는다. 해석: 기본 formatted 연결과 unformatted READ가 충돌한다. 실행 결과는 확인하지 않았다. |
| F200-07 | NWS=11: 기압 단위 주장 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:84` `- Surface pressure: millibars`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:101` `PRESSURE{m H2O} = PRESSURE{millibars}/(100*GRAVITY*DENSITY of H2O)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4165` `RHOWATG100=RHOWAT0*G*100.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4227` `PRN2(N)=P1*D1(N)+P2*D2(N)+P3*D3(N)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4228` `PRN2(N)=PRN2(N)/RHOWATG100` | 불일치 | 문서는 입력 기압을 millibar로 적는다. 문서의 식과 코드는 기압을 100*RHOWAT0*G로 나눈다. 해석: millibar를 Pa로 바꾸는 100배 변환과 반대이다. NWS=16의 millibar 변환은 MET-43에 적었다. |
| F200-08 | NWS=11: 기압 변환식 자체 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:101` `PRESSURE{m H2O} = PRESSURE{millibars}/(100*GRAVITY*DENSITY of H2O)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4165` `RHOWATG100=RHOWAT0*G*100.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4228` `PRN2(N)=PRN2(N)/RHOWATG100` | 일치 | 코드는 문서의 식을 그대로 구현한다. 입력 단위 주장과 식의 충돌은 F200-07에서 별도로 판정했다. |
| F200-09 | NWS=11: WTIMINC당 dataset 수 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:74` `- Each file contains 8 data sets spaced 3 hours apart (WTIMINC = 10800 sec)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:109` `- For NWS=11, each file contains 8 data sets for a total of 1 day of data per file`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:111` `- For NWS=11, 8 sets of meteorological data must be provided every WTIMINC in each file` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4205` `1100 IF(IDSETFLG.EQ.8) CLOSE(IEXT)` | 불일치 | 문서 마지막 문장은 WTIMINC마다 여덟 dataset을 요구한다. 같은 문서의 앞부분은 WTIMINC 간격의 dataset 여덟 개를 하루 파일에 요구한다. 코드는 IDSETFLG=8에서 파일을 닫는다. |
| F200-10 | NWS=11: -I 입력 경로 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst:11` ``` ``-I INPUTDIR`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst:12` `Look for input files in the directory 'INPUTDIR' instead of looking in the directory where the ADCIRC command was issued.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4184` `WRITE(FNAME1,'(A5,I3.3)') 'fort.',IEXT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4187` `call openFileForRead(IEXT,FNAME1,errorIO)` | 불일치 | 코드는 FNAME1을 현재 작업 디렉터리에 상대적인 이름으로 연다. 코드는 INPUTDIR를 붙이지 않는다. |

### 2.20 fort.22·221·222·223·224: OWI ASCII NWS=±12

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| OWI-01 | fort.22: NWSET·NWBS·DWM | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:21` ``\| :ref:`NWSET` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:22` ``\| :ref:`NWBS` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:23` ``\| :ref:`DWM` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:28` `-  **NWSET** is the number of nested grid overlays`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:29` `-  **NWBS** is the number of "blank" (constant 0 m/s wind speed and 1013mb`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:30` `pressure) timesteps to insert`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:31` `-  **DWM** is a wind multiplier to modify the wind speed magnitudes. Generally`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:32` `this should be set to 1 if using inputs sourced from OWI or if winds can be`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:33` `considered "marine exposure", with an averaging period between 10-min and`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:34` `3-hrs at a height of 10 meters.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:192` `read(iounit,*,err=99999,end=99998,iostat=errorIO) numSets`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:195` `if(numSets.EQ.0)then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:196` `call check_owi_err(1,"number of wind sets must not be zero",fname,iounit)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:201` `read(iounit,*,err=99999,end=99998,iostat=errorIO) numBlankSnaps`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:214` `read(iounit,*,err=99999, end=99998, iostat=errorIO) windMultiplier`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:220` `allocate(owi(1:abs(numSets)))` | 일치 | 코드는 세 헤더를 순서대로 읽는다. NWSET=0이면 종료한다. DWM은 풍속 배율이다. 문서의 should 조건은 권고로 유지한다. |
| OWI-02 | NWBS 부호·초기 blank·skip | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:61` `If a NWBS is set to a positive number, NWBS specifies the number of blank snaps`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:62` `to be inserted before any information is read from fort.22[1-4]. This is useful`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:77` `If NWBS is set to a negative number, then NWBS determines how many snaps in the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:78` `fort.22[1-4] should be skipped before the values in the files are used. This is` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:205` `if(numBlankSnaps.LT.0) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:206` `numSkipSnaps = ABS(numBlankSnaps)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:207` `numBlankSnaps = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:208` `else`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:209` `numSkipSnaps = 0` | 일치 | 음수 NWBS에서 코드는 ABS(NWBS)개 snapshot을 건너뛴다. 양수 NWBS는 초기 blank snapshot 수이다. |
| OWI-03 | 기본 파일 이름·중첩 우선순위·단위 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:40` `-  *fort.221*: Largest scale pressure (mb) fields`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:41` `-  *fort.222*: Largest scale wind vector (m/s) fields`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:42` `-  *fort.223*: Finer scale pressure (mb) fields`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:43` `-  *fort.224*: Finer scale wind vector (m/s) fields`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:95` `#. When the basin and region scale grids are both used in ADCIRC, data from the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:96` `region scale grid takes precedence over data from the basin scale grid in the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws12.rst:97` `areas where the two grids overlap.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:888` `this(1)%pressure_file = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//"/fort.221"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:889` `this(1)%wind_file = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//"/fort.222"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:893` `this(2)%pressure_file = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//"/fort.223"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:894` `this(2)%wind_file = TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//"/fort.224"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:351` `PRN(i)  = 100.d0*PP/RHOWATG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:355` `WVNX(i) = uu * windMultiplier`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:356` `WVNY(i) = vv * windMultiplier`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:312` `DO S = 1,numSets` | 일치 | 코드는 기본 파일 이름을 GBLINPUTDIR에 붙인다. 코드는 기압 mb에 100을 곱하고 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 코드는 풍속에 windMultiplier를 곱한다. 중첩 처리에서 뒤의 격자 값이 앞의 격자 값을 덮어쓴다. |

Wind speeds set to zero and the pressure field",`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind.F:621` `&      " to its background value.")` | 일치 | 데이터 READ가 EOF에 이르면 코드는 blank snapshot을 만든다. 코드는 풍속 0과 배경 기압을 사용한다. should be safely caught라는 문서의 권고를 보장 문장으로 바꾸지 않았다. |

### 2.21 fort.22.nc: OWI netCDF NWS=±13

| ID | 파일·레코드·필드 | 문서 위치와 원문 | 코드 읽기·검사·변환 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NC13-01 | 파일 형식·파일 이름 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:65` `**NetCDF4 File** contents:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:67` `-  Group(s) – 1 group per grid or overlay`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:68` `-  Variables – U10/V10, PSFC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:69` `-  Dimensions – time, yi, xi` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:530` `NWS13ColdStartString = "99999999.999999"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:531` `NWS13File = "fort.22.nc"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:532` `NWS13GroupForPowell = "0"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:533` `NWS13WindMultiplier = "1.0"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:262` `call check_err(NF90_OPEN(TRIM(ADJUSTL(NWS13File)),NF90_NOWRITE,NC_ID))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:265` `call check_err(NF90_GET_ATT(NC_ID,NF90_GLOBAL,"group_order",GroupOrder))` | 일치 | 코드는 NWS13File의 netCDF 파일을 연다. 기본 이름은 fort.22.nc이다. 기본 배율 문자열은 1.0이다. 기본 Powell 그룹 문자열은 0이다. |
| NC13-02 | NWS=±13의 입력 종류 설명 | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:93` `* - 13, -13`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:94` `- Ramped meteorological forcing`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:95` `- Similar to NWS=5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs over a user-specified time period`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:96` `- fort.22`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:308` `**NWS = 13, -13**`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst:309` `Similar to NWS = 5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs. Wind velocity and atmospheric pressure are read in at all grid nodes from the Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File. The positive/negative convention for time reference is the same as with NWS = 5/-5. The meteorological forcing is ramped up over a user-specified time period using the WRAMP parameter.` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2041` `WTIME1=STATIM*86400.D0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2045` `CALL NWS13INIT(0.D0,W)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:2046` `CALL NWS13GET(WTIME1,WVNX1,WVNY1,PRN1,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:262` `call check_err(NF90_OPEN(TRIM(ADJUSTL(NWS13File)),NF90_NOWRITE,NC_ID))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:265` `call check_err(NF90_GET_ATT(NC_ID,NF90_GLOBAL,"group_order",GroupOrder))` | 불일치 | NWS 종합 페이지는 NWS=5 형식의 fort.22와 WRAMP를 설명한다. 전용 nws13.rst와 코드는 OWI netCDF 그룹 입력을 사용한다. 코드 판은 v56.2.1이다. 이 불일치는 현행 rst 사이에도 있다. |
| NC13-03 | group_order | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:74` `-  *group_order*: space separated list of group names reflecting their`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:75` `appropriate rank/order` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:265` `call check_err(NF90_GET_ATT(NC_ID,NF90_GLOBAL,"group_order",GroupOrder))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:269` `IF(INDEX(TRIM(GroupOrder)," ").EQ.1)THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/owiwind_netcdf.F:273` `TempOrder(1:GroupOrderLen-1) = GroupOrder(2:GroupOrderLen)` | 일치 | 코드는 공백으로 구분한 그룹 이름 목록을 읽는다. 코드는 목록 순서를 사용한다. |
